Chapter 2901: Beyond Reincarnation

“Isee.”

From the depths of Li Yao’s soul, another bolt of lightning cut through the darkness and illuminated his understanding, “Is the so-called ‘reincarnation’ the process of ‘digesting and absorbing a proud and stubborn soul’ in the deep, illusionary hypnosis?”

With such insight, Li Yao would be able to see the essence of the games of the Fuxis more clearly.

In the beginning of the reincarnation, it seemed that ‘Fuxi’, the super crystal processor of the primeval era a thousand years ago, had just been woken up by Black Star Emperor Wu Yingqi and created a lot of puppets.

The virtual human being that had been born based on the souls of Lu Qingchen, Chu Zhixiao, and the rest of them had spent the rest of his life as a ‘battle puppet’. He had received all kinds of stimulation and received ever-changing feedback.

Countless precious and confidential data flowed out of the core of his soul and was further grasped by Fuxi.

Fuxi took the opportunity to insert a lot of its data into the core of his soul.

If too much data was injected into a hypnotization so that the enemy would forget that he was Lu Qingchen, it would be too difficult for the enemy to believe that he was a Fuxi. Lu Qingchen might be awakened and resist, too.

However, the process of ‘injection of data’ and ‘deep hypnosis’ could be broken down into dozens of cycles, or even hundreds of cycles. If the process was slightly altered each time, it was possible that the process would gradually change without triggering any resistance.

In the next reincarnation, time jumped to the era of the Star Ocean Imperium ten thousand years ago. The fleet loyal to the Supreme Emperor and the rebels of the Mad Armageddon, Blood God, were engaged in a great war in the sea of stars, dying every orange and ivory star crimson.

At this point in time, Lu Qingchen had been reincarnated many times. Sometimes he was a Cultivator loyal to the Supreme Emperor, and sometimes he was a rebel loyal to the Mad Armageddon, Blood God. He was engaged in fierce battles in the sea of stars and exploring the depths of the earth’s crust. He

had fought his way from the center of the sea of stars to the edge of the sea of stars. He had experienced countless loves, hates, schemes, and betrayals. He had been blown to pieces time and time again, only to fall to the ground and cry loudly.

During the process, many of his life experiences had subtle connections with ‘Fuxi’, the super crystal processor from the primeval era.

“Huh. Judging from the scenes, the Fuxi Clan has been itching to be resurrected since the civil war of the Star Ocean Imperium ten thousand years ago!”

Li Yao was analyzing the core database of the Fuxis with all his attention when he suddenly found some very interesting historical files. He thought quickly and realized what was going on. Right. Wu Yingqi waking up the Fuxis was not a coincidence but intentional.

“It is estimated that Blood God, the Mad Armageddon from ten thousand years ago, had a certain relationship with Fuxi. It was only with the help of Fuxi that he was able to get rid of the Supreme Emperor’s control and become an independent entity with the ability to compete with the Supreme Emperor!

“Why did Fuxi help the Blood God upgrade to “Mad Armageddon’? The answer is obvious, too. He was trying to revive himself!

“However, ten thousand years ago, its scheme failed. The Supreme Emperor and the Mad Armageddon, Blood God, perished together. Naturally, nobody was able to activate it.

“Therefore, the project was postponed for ten thousand years. It was not until the remnant soul of Blood God, the Mad Armageddon, found Wu Yinggi that the mission to ‘wake up the Fux’ was completed by the Black Star Emperor!

“How magnificent was the civil war of the Star Ocean Imperium ten thousand years ago? How many secrets were hidden? Where did the so-called ‘Supreme Emperor’ come from? Where is he right now? Is his ‘Gold Throne’ really an ancient tomb? What is the answer to all this?”

Holding back the surging waves of his soul, Li Yao continued observing and analyzing quietly.

After more than ten reincarnations, Lu Qingchen’s soul gradually returned to the ancient times.

One time, he tured into a human warrior who gritted his teeth in the darkness for decades in the ‘30,000-year Dark Age’ when the demons savaged the sea of stars.

In another reincarnation, he turned into a ruler of the demon race and chased after the resistance warriors in the sea of stars with a butcher’s knife.

In the collision of blood and iron, schemes and betrayals, he was more and more confused and muddleheaded. He also leaked more secrets deep inside his soul.

‘When his soul finally ‘reincarnated’ to the ancient Cultivation world, he seemed to have been completely lost.

Li Yao had watched him go through multiple reincarnations as a refugee, as an official of the imperial court, as a soldier or a general, or as a sword immortal who was singing with a sword in his hand. Some of them were elegant and elegant, some were miserable and miserable, some were extravagant

and extravagant—no matter what his identity was, he hardly looked up at the sky anymore and hardly ever thought of the distant past. He used to be Lu Qingchen, the Lu Qingchen who dared to fire at the deities and devils!

After countless reincarnations, his soul was like an onion that had been peeled layer by layer by the Fuxi, exposing the most important and fragile core.

In the meantime, the real Lu Qingchen had been buried and tampered with by the Fuxi, who had injected countless complicated data into it.

In the end, the core database of the Fuxis was deeply embedded into the core of Lu Qingchen’s soul in mysterious ways. A lot of raw data and core logic flooded in crazily and were about to be melded into an inseparable whole.

The absorption was about to be completed.

This was the true meaning of reincarnation.

Li Yao looked at Lu Qingchen, who was drifting in the endless reincarnation of the Sea of Desire, as if he were looking at his twisted reflection through a dirty pond.

Earth. Countless Earths. Countless reincarnation cycles.

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

Countless brand-new, extremely painful memory pieces popped up on the surface of his soul, making Li Yao feel that his nonexistent head had turned into two large ones.

He suddenly felt that he was more than just an earthling.

No. It should be said that he was indeed ‘Earthling Li Yao’. But Li Yao had experienced countless reincarnations and was not just an ordinary mechanic and racer living on the modern Earth.

In some memory pieces, he saw that he looked like a soldier in a forest with colorful mud smeared on his face and a dagger in his hand.

In some other memory pieces, he had returned to a vintage era just like Lu Qingchen, turning into a farmer who was working at sunrise and resting at sunset.

There were also countless different memories, different lives, different love, hatred, and emotions. They were essentially the same but not the same world view. The messy memory pieces had completely shattered his brain.

“What—what does this mean? Have I experienced countless reincarnations just like Lu Qingchen? Even though my reincarnations are many times more delicate and complicated, they are essentially the same?”

Li Yao did not dare to think any further. “Let’s say that the reincarnation in front of me is the reincarnation of Fuxi studying, analyzing, and dissecting Lu Qingchen’s soul through hypnosis time and time again. Then, what is my reincarnation and the reincarnation of thousands of Earthlings worth?

“My origin. Who was the original me?

“and what were the thousands upon thousands of Earthlings like in the beginning?

No wonder the Vulture Plan was implemented.

No wonder they wanted to destroy the Earth!

“Concentrate. It’s useless to overthink right now!”

When Li Yao was caught in the endless loop of thinking and could not free himself, the mental devil said solemnly, “This should be the last world at the end of the reincarnation!

Li Yao was greatly alarmed. He scanned with his telepathic thoughts and found that the world that Lu Qingchen’s soul was about to enter was made of endless golden data streams whose form and structure were much more delicate than those of the previous dozens of reincarnations.

It was like a glittering golden apple. Fuxi must’ve spent a lot of computational ability to create and maintain it.

“The virtual world involves the most critical ‘meta-logic’ in the core database of the Fuxi Clan. It should be the source of everything!

The mental devil replied, “All the most critical meta-logic, infrastructure, and initial data of the Fuxis, including the highest permissions to control them, should be hidden in this virtual world!

“Lu Qingchen’s soul has been mostly dissected and dissected. As long as the last reincarnation is completed and he is fully digested, the Fuxi will be awakened and become an invincible existence that is absolutely free and beyond any control!

The time in this virtual world was reversed by hundreds of thousands of years. It was the end of the primeval war, the era when the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan were about to run out of oil and their civilization to extinction.

It was also the time when Fuxi gradually deviated from his original mission, or rather, the era when he could explain and distort his own mission, through countless self-attacks and the activation of his authority.

“We cannot stand by and watch the souls of Lu Qingchen, Chu Zhixiao, and the rest of them be devoured by the Fuxi. There is still time. We must stop it!”

Li Yao said anxiously.

“Yes, but this is the core world of the Fuxis. Causing trouble here is hundreds of times more difficult than flying freely in the hundreds of ordinary worlds just now. If anything goes wrong, the Fuxis will discover us.

The mental devil hesitated. “Unless… something happens to reduce the computational ability of the Fuxis and make them unable to maintain 100% of the perfection and solidness of the core world, thereby creating loopholes that we can exploit.

BOOM!

Hardly had the mental devil finished its sentence when thunderous tides suddenly came close. Tiny ripples appeared on the surface of the immaculate golden world, too. Many parts of it were much dimmer than before.

“This is—”

Li Yao was dazed for a moment, before he burst into ecstasy. “It’s a space jump! Fuxi has drained most of his computational ability and is performing an overwhelming super large-scale space jump. It finally couldn’t hold it any longer and launched an attack on the capital city! Maybe the reinforcements

of the federation have arrived, or maybe Lei Chenghu and Boss Bai have secured a decisive victory. Either way, Fuxi has to start the war. It is like an arrow that has been nocked and has to be fired!”

“Now is the time. Let’s go. Let’s transcend reincarnation and fight to the death against the Fuxis.. Let’s wake up Lu Qingchen and Chu Zhixiao, and… ourselves!”

Chapter 2902: A Thousand-Year Ending

At the edge of the Empyrean Terminus Sector and the planet, in the boundless darkness, a lot of round crystal cameras and self-detonating magnetic mines were scattered. The runes carved on their surfaces were emitting lackluster colors while they transmitted simple data and commands. Sometimes,

they congregated into a cluster, and sometimes, they spread out into a splendid network, floating, cruising, and wriggling.

If the universe was a warm and humid ancient ocean, the cold lumps made of metal and crystals might have met the definition of ‘cells’ in the beginning. They were like a blanket made of primitive fungi that gradually grew into multiple-cell life forms and embarked on a new, marvelous journey of

evolution.

If the universe could be compared to the interior of a gargantuan ‘super creature’, the lumps would be like vigilant white blood cells that were monitoring the slightest anomaly in the space within millions of square kilometers without missing the tiniest ripple.

“Chirp chirp chirp.”

“Squeak squeak squeak.”

Beep. Beep. Beep. Beep. Beep. Beep. Beep. Beep.

‘The commands that the human beings gave them were dissected into complicated waves and simple symbols, which flowed in the thousands of circuits of the crystal chips that they controlled and transmitted to a place far away.

Occasionally, when they left the coverage of the Spiritual Nexus and entered the desolate land without a network, the special instructions and programs stored in their memory chips would be automatically activated and grant them a certain degree of autonomy, so that the iron lumps without

consciousness could carry out all kinds of ‘mission’.

—f we get rid of the limitations of carbon-based intelligent life and look at it from a higher perspective, why can’t the scene in front of us be the beginning of a certain primitive life form that is slowly awakening and stepping onto the road of life?

However, such a start was interrupted by the sudden alarms.

In the depths of the sea of stars more than 74 million kilometers away from the Empyrean Terminus Sector—as far as the universe was concerned—it was only the distance of a finger. The previously stable three-dimensional vacuum seemed to have been assaulted by the Hellfire-fire from the

four-dimensional space. Red, orange, blue, and green halos of different colors appeared out of thin air. The halos on the plane quickly bulged into bubbles, which expanded and expanded rapidly. In the end, they exploded silently, revealing immeasurable black holes at the center of which were scattered

stars that seemed to lead to another universe billions of lightyears away.

A cluster of wormholes millions of kilometers in diameter was born and grew larger and larger.

Space ripples spread out like surging tides.

A lot of materials were about to break the impulse and arrive at the periphery of the Empyrean Terminus Sector.

Ina moment, sensing the intense waves of the space ripples and the abnormal data that a high-density object was about to arrive, all the scout crystal cameras and magnetic mines nearby were woken up.

Like fungi that had discovered their prey or the white blood cells of the intruders, they fully activated their power units and spurted dazzling exhaust flames toward the coordinates of the wormhole.

The scout crystal cameras were trying to get as close to the enemy as possible so that more data could be collected and sent back to the rear before they were torn apart by the space ripples. The magnetic mines were rushing to the center of the cluster of wormholes and melted into the magnetic field of

the high-mass, high-density objects. They exploded at the last moment, releasing the most brilliant brilliance of their ‘life’ and disrupting the enemy’s jump.

Right now, the scout crystal cameras and the magnetic mines did not understand the significance of ‘life’. They were merely carrying out the orders from the depths of Empyrean Terminus dutifully.

Just like the fungi, bacteria, and cells in the beginning, it was impossible for them to understand what ‘life’ was. They simply obeyed the instructions named ‘instincts’.

The wormholes appeared in various directions of the Empyreal Terminus Sector. Tens of thousands of magnetic mines were activated at the same time and charged at the wormholes closest to them, turning themselves into brilliant fireballs like the warriors who had risked their lives to stop the

wormholes from expanding further and tearing apart the starships that had only jumped halfway.

For a moment, the entire sea of stars was illuminated. The super radiation resulting from the destruction of the wormholes eclipsed billions of stars. Even the observatory on the surface of Empyrean Terminus could observe the burning Heavenly Fire in the periphery of the galaxy, which had congregated

into a burning river!

In just one hour, countless wormholes that had just been opened were seriously jammed or even annihilated.

But for the biggest invasion in the past thousand years, such automatic defense was far from enough.

Fuxi had collected a lot of intelligence about the defense system of the capital city and inferred thousands of different changes. Such regular tactics were within its calculation.

As a result, the first batch of starships to jump through the wormholes were not the main force warships such as the super arsenals. They were not even starships. They were just small-scale self-detonation magical equipment similar to the self-driving space shuttles and fully-automatic magnetic

mines.

They were also made of cold metals and crystals. Their cost was low, and they were abundant. They could search for and approach the densest coordinates of the enemy automatically before they exploded. The only difference was that there was a small cockpit in the batch of ‘self-detonating mines’

refined by the Fuxis. The living, flesh-and-blood puppet warriors could crouch inside like babies, lock onto the enemy, and sprint forward. Considering the simplicity of such magical equipment, the lack of fuel, and the mode of attack, it was almost impossible to retrieve them once they were launched

into the sea of stars from the mothership. The death rate of the pilot was 100%. Therefore, even the brutal imperial army was not equipped with similar magical equipment. Only the system of the Covenant Alliance could find enough soldiers who were highly brainwashed and did not fear death.

It was a silent war, not to mention an ‘unhuman’ war. When the warriors of the Holy Alliance sat on dozens of tons of high-explosive crystals and charged at the iron defense line opposite them emotionlessly, detonating the crystals and their own flesh and blood, their souls were highly frozen and sealed,

too. They did not fear death at all.

On the other hand, the self-detonation of a magnetic mine on the side of the Covenant Alliance could trigger overwhelming tides of spiritual energy, which would interfere with the transmission of information and the spiritual energy circuits nearby and disable all the high-precision magical equipment nearby.

‘The magnetic mines, scout crystal cameras, and self-driving shuttles of the imperium were all eclipsed by the magnetic storm. They lost their impetus and could only move forward based on inertia until they left the target area and turned into lonely dust.

However, the magnetic mines of the members of the Star Glory Federation were controlled by a suicide squad member. Even though most of the automatic functions had been lost, it did not stop the squad member from rushing to the general attack zone. Then, another spiritual tsunami came!

Through such insane tactics, the main fleet of the Covenant Alliance managed to maintain the normal opening and expansion of most of the wormholes and established solid beachheads outside of the wormholes. No matter how hard the defenders bombarded them, the biggest wormholes were finally

broken through. They were like the eyes of a devil that were constantly opening, allowing the largest main force warship to pass through.

Therefore, the eyes of the demon quickly drew complicated, transparent lines that constituted the outline and structure of a warship. They were delicate and clear. In the end, they were torn apart like thin black gauze, and magnificent starships appeared one after another.

The spiritual interference starships, the super arsenals, the cruising motherships, and the star fortresses were still affected by the ripples when they passed through the four-dimensional space, making their appearances in the three-dimensional universe even more unpredictable. They seemed to be

wreathed in intense smoke and the dominating aura of the space beasts. The dominating aura alone was enough to tear apart several layers of defense lines and reach Empyrean Terminus effortlessly.

When the five supreme fortresses, which represented the highest combat ability of the Holy Accord Alliance, marched out of the periphery of the Empyreal Terminus Sector under the protection of many starships and unleashed their killing intent, there was no doubt anymore.

It was not a feint, a distraction, or a trick to fool the enemy. It was indeed the main fleet of the Star Glory Federation that was coming at the imperium in full strength!

In the control center deep below the Gold Crystal Tower of Empyrean Terminus.

Xiao Ming and Wen Wen were both sitting on a control chair made of countless crystal processors and crystal cables. Their eyes were glittering as if endless data was flashing in them.

Li Yao’s body was still intact in the hibernation cabin, but there was a thick crystal cable connected to the back of his head. The crystal cable extended all the way to the ground and connected to the countless communication stations floating in space through a transmitter. In such a way, if Li Yao’s soul

returned to the Empyrean Terminus Sector, it would be possible to return to his body through the stations, which was a slim hope for Xiao Ming and Wen Wen.

However, right now, the two little guys could not care less where Li Yao’s soul was.

They were all deeply awed by the shocking number of the main fleet of the Covenant Alliance and their determination to fight.

“How many starships are there exactly? Our crystal cameras can’t calculate all of them!

Xiao Ming exclaimed.

“The people of the Covenant Alliance seem to have gathered all their starships in a small battlefield. What are they up to exactly? Have they abandoned the defense of all the worlds behind them?”

Wen Wen did not understand, either.

According to the preliminary estimation of the number and combat ability, the capital city alone is definitely not enough to defend the place. It seems that they are not here to attack but to destroy the place!

Xiao Ming came to a desperate conclusion.

The two little guys looked at each other and saw the rapidly exploding blood vessels in the bottom of their eyes. Such a complicated and chaotic situation had surpassed the limits of their computational ability in the blink of an eye. They were barely able to maintain it on their own. The heads of the two

little guys were about to explode.

“Inform the young emperor and the queen mother that the main fleet of the Star Glory Federation has really shown up.. Their strength is far beyond our imagination!

Chapter 2903: Three Good News!

On the surface of Empyrean Terminus, the alarms swept over the entire planet like a burning heatwave.

Countless steel dragons woke up and rushed to every important city from the military base. Countless skyscrapers lowered their iron armor and covered the fragile glass windows with rumbling noises. The earth was tom apart, and countless air defense cannons rose up, locking every coordinate deep

inside the atmosphere and making sure that any enemy who broke into the atmosphere would be torn into pieces at the most vulnerable moment.

Not to mention that a lot of Immortal Cultivators had appeared in the streets, military bases, and even the imperial city, fully armed and fearless.

This was the Empyrean Terminus Sector, the Empyrean Terminus Planet, the imperial capital, and the thousand-year beating heart of the Imperium of True Human Beings!

Although the families of the four electors had risen and the imperial capital had declined over the past five hundred years, the heritage of the past thousand years was still there. Countless Immortal Cultivators still considered the place to be the last line of defense.

Hiu! Hiu! Hiu! Hiu!

Countless colorful pillars of smoke rose vertically and pierced into the vacuum of the universe. Most of them were Immortal Cultivators who were wearing crystal suits and carrying small starships. Their carriers might not be able to perform long-distance space jumps, but they were more than enough to

shuttle through a galaxy. Even if their carriers were blown up ina fierce battle in the sea of stars, as long as their crystal suits were intact, they could still return to Empyrean Terminus or land on every planet and satellite nearby with the attraction force of the planets.

After all, the imperial army had established military bases on every solid planet of the Empyrean Terminus Sector. Wherever they landed, they would receive effective rest and supplies.

It was also the biggest advantage of the defense team in an internal battle.

Therefore, under the protection of the forest of swords and spears and billions of sparks, the citizens and the Immortal Cultivators of the capital city were still very confident, even though the enemy was coming at them unstoppably. They were ready to fight until the end and perish together with

Empyrean Terminus.

Moreover, with the crazy construction of the underground natives over the past few months, the level-four defense network of Empyrean Terminus had been mostly established, too. If the situation was truly out of control, and all the planets and star fortresses of the Empyrean Terminus Sector were

occupied, the Immortal Cultivators would still be able to retreat to the cities and underground and fight the enemy in every street, building, mine, and fortress brutally.

After all, their enemy was the Holy Accord Alliance, the terminator of the human civilization!

On the imperial city, flags were flying, The three-star emblem on the flags was like a bouncing flame that was about to burn the entire world.

Emperor Wu Yingling—Li Jialing—stood on the gate tower and looked down at the golden phalanx made of thousands of elite soldiers under the protection of the three-star flag of lightning.

The tens of thousands of elites were all the best experts selected from the millions of soldiers of the imperial guards. Li Jialing and Li Linghai had spared no expense and poured all the resources they collected from the four major electors’ families into them, thereby improving their Cultivation to a whole

new level.

Naturally, their loyalty to the ‘new empire’ and the emperor was unshakable, too.

Li Jialing himself was wearing a set of gold lion armor, too. The two shoulders of the armor were raised high, making him look like two lions that had opened their bloody mouths, making his young body look even more magnificent, like the incarnation of the blood-stained flag behind him.

Right now, the entire imperial capital knew that a great enemy was coming. The urgent alarms gradually died down. The spacious square was silent. The only thing that could be heard was the blowing wind and the fluttering flags.

“Creators of the new imperium, defenders of the civilization of mankind, and my most loyal warriors, I have three pieces of good news to share with you!

Li Jialing looked around and let the silence simmer for a long time. It was not until all the eyes of the imperial guards were focused on him and burning hot that he extended three fingers and said unhurriedly, “Firstly, three hours ago, the command center received the latest battle report from the east.

The rebel army under the command of General Lei Chenghu has just defeated the last coalition fleet of the remnants of the pseudo-Marquis in the Battle of Jade Cauldron and blown up more than 43,500 main warships. More than 150,000 of the enemy’s main warships have lost their mobility and defense

ability and become the captives of our army. The biggest resource supply base and weapon factory of the remnants of the pseudo-Marquis have been restored and bathed in the brilliance of the new imperium!

“The victory of the ‘Battle of Jade Cauldron’ means that the remnants of the pseudo-Marquis have completely lost the last bit of mobility to travel in the sea of stars. From now on, they can only curl up on the lone island-like planets and struggle for their last breath. They will no longer be able to cause the

slightest bit of interference to the brilliance of our new empire! In fact, when the news that our army has secured a great victory spreads, countless planets under the reign of the remnants of the pseudo-Marquis will be agitated and rebel. They will raise their flags and announce that ‘anyway’, they will

return to our embrace!

“Because of the thoroughness of the plan, the three soldiers, as well as the internal strife of the enemy, did not have the slightest bit of morale in the first place. It was a satisfying and devastating victory. General Lei Chenghu’s rebels barely suffered any losses in the ‘Battle of Jade Cauldron’. Also, with the

captive warships and the resources of the entire Jade Cauldron Sector, their capability has increased instead of decreased. Their morale has soared, too. In a few days, they will finish their rest and reinforce the capital city, killing the enemy in the iron defense line of the capital city!

It was indeed great news. Countless Immortal Cultivators were greatly shocked.

The greatest concern of the new empire was to fight on two fronts against the members of the Star Glory Federation and the remnants of the four electors.

Right now, one of the battle lines had secured a decisive victory. All they needed to do was to hold the line until Lei Chenghu returned gloriously under the command of the rebel army. Then, the hope of victory would blossom!

In an instant, the eyes of countless Immortal Cultivators lit up.

“Second.

Li Jialing said solemnly, “Reinforcements from the Star Glory Federation at the edge of the sea of stars, namely the territory of ‘Black Wind King’ Li Yao, are also being gathered. You may consider the federal reinforcements to be an upgraded version of the ‘Black Wind Fleet’, which is stronger and more

loyal!

“Because of the long distance and the enormous size of the reinforcements, they did not arrive in time. But please believe me, everybody, that the reinforcements will break out of the enemy’s vulnerable rear at the most critical moment!

After he said that, the golden phalanx buzzed again.

With so many smart Immortal Cultivators, it was possible that they had guessed the background of ‘Black Wind King Li Yao! and the ‘Upgraded Black Wind Fleet’. At the very least, they knew that the reinforcements were made of a lot of Cultivators.

However, it did not matter. The conflicts between Cultivators and Immortal Cultivators were ‘contradictions among human beings’ after all. The emotionless, tearless, mechanical people of the Sanctuary were the common enemy.

To put it bluntly, tuning the Immortal Cultivators into Cultivators might hurt them, and they might feel uncomfortable, but it was not enough to kill them.

However, it would be a fate worse than death if the Immortal Cultivators were turned into puppets without emotions, memories, and self-awareness.

Besides, the size of the two parties was obvious. As long as the urgent issue at hand was resolved and the new empire could gain a firm foothold or even control most of the resources in the center of the sea of stars, they would be invincible no matter what the game was.

Therefore, it was also a piece of great news. All the elites of the imperial guards were overjoyed.

“Thirdly, and also the best news, according to the data sent back by the scout crystal cameras deployed in the periphery of the Empyreal Terminus Sector, the main fleet of the Star Glory Federation is far larger in number and combat ability than we expected. The members of the Star Glory Federation

have gone all out this time. They are planning to perish together with us!

LiJialing extended his third finger and uttered the deafening ‘good news’. Before the elites of the imperial guards realized what was going on, he continued shouting, “Do you know why I call this ‘good news’? It’s simple. It’s because our enemy is at the end of his rope and has no other options except to

take a desperate gamble!

“Marshal Lei Chenghu was right about one year ago. The pressure that the Holy Alliance is under is a hundred times greater than that of the Star Ocean Imperium. The Holy Alliance has drained the last bit of flesh inside its body and is about to collapse because of its own weight. It cannot hold on any

longer.

“That’s why the Holy Alliance is so insane as to gather forces far beyond our imagination and launch a final counterattack, hoping to prolong their meaningless lives!

“The scale of the invasion fleet means that the people of the Star Glory Federation have abandoned the defense at the rear. Every planet of theirs has tured into a shaved monkey, shivering in the cold wind!

“Therefore, what happened next is very simple.

“If we win, as long as we secure the victory of the defense of the capital city and join hands with the reinforcements of the main fleet of the Star Glory Federation in the most sacred heart of the Thousand Year Imperium, we will be the final winner of the Thousand Year War! By then, every world and every

planet in the rear of the Star Glory Federation will no longer have an organized mobile force to stop us from creating greater glory! At that time, the citizens of the Star Glory Federation on every barren planet will be awakened, and they will be waiting for the arrival of the army of the imperium and the

hope of their survival like the rescuers! At that time, the territory of the entire Star Glory Federation and the remnants of the fake marquis will return to our embrace. Our contributions will far exceed those of the Black Star Emperor a thousand years ago and reach the level of the Star Ocean Imperium ten

thousand years ago. As for you, the most loyal warriors of the Star Glory Federation and the most determined defenders of the human civilization, your names will be carved on the brightest stars and brought to every corner of the universe by the brilliance of the stars. You will be praised and admired by

billions of people thousands of years later!

Chapter 2904: Glory And Redemption!

The provocative words were surprisingly sincere when they came out of Li Jialing’s mouth. His eyes were like flowing flames, too. Wherever they went, the Immortal Cultivators could not help but feel their blood boiling. They seemed to be reminded of their ambition when they swore to rebuild their glory

when the first batch of Immortal Cultivators was born a thousand years ago.

“Long live Your Majesty! Long live the imperium! Long live the civilization of mankind!”

The tens of thousands of elites of the imperial guards in the golden phalanx roared in an earthshaking voice as if they were one person.

shua!

LiJialing unsheathed the long sword on his waist and stabbed it into the sky where the clouds and mist were surging. He shouted, “Today’s battle is not just a battle of honor, but also a battle of atonement! Do you know what a heinous crime we have committed? No. It is not deception, scheming, or

plundering. It is not the cruel and merciless law of the jungle. In the long river of human civilization, such details are insignificant.

“Itis not a sin for the Immortal Cultivators to own most of the resources. The real sin is that we failed to fulfil our promise, we failed to push the civilization of mankind to a new peak, and we failed to build an invincible imperium! We were too weak to the point that the enemy marched in and stabbed us

right in the heart. That is our only and deepest sin. We and the rest of you have committed the sin of weakness!

“Survival of the fittest and survival of the fittest are rules that we’ve been adhering to for thousands of years. But do you think that the Immortal Cultivators today are strong enough to enjoy all the resources with a clear conscience and face all the enemies inside and outside the universe fearlessly?

“Of course, we are all very clear that it was not us who caused the weakness of the Immortal Cultivators today, but the so-called ‘Four Electors’ and the corrupt nobles who corrupted the real Immortal Cultivators with cowardice, selfishness, and cheap tricks!

“But now, we are the masters of Empyrean Terminus and Empyrean Terminus. We are the founders and defenders of the new imperium. We are the true successors and pioneers of the path of immortality. It is our duty to carry the sin of weakness on our shoulders. Then, we will wash away the sin bit by

bit with our blades, our hot blood, and our soul, wiping away the gloom on the path of immortality so that the path of immortality will flourish in the next thousand, ten thousand, or even a hundred thousand years!

“Therefore, unsheathe your blades and summon your courage. Let the ugly enemies open their eyes and see clearly what a real Immortal Cultivator is. Let the senseless walking dead shiver in our roars!

“Below your feet, this is the burial place of the old imperium and the starting point of the new one and the new era. Go now, my warriors, my… Fellow Cultivators. Create the greatest era that belongs to you, the new imperium, and the civilization of mankind!

‘When Li Jialing’s roar reached its peak, he slashed the golden blade in his hand down. A streak of brightness immediately rushed out like a flood and turned into hundreds of lions that were raging in midair. Then, it tumed into glittering ‘raindrops’ and rained on the crystal suits of every elite soldier,

displaying the glamorous effect of blossoming golden scales.

Although there were no substantial changes, the splendid special effects still dazzled all the elites of the imperial guards, and their morale soared.

“Tm willing to die for Your Majesty!”

“We are the real Immortal Cultivators. The new era of the Immortal Cultivators must begin now!

“So what if the main fleet of the Covenant Alliance is large in number? Reinforcements are arriving soon. All we need to do is defend to the last breath. How can the strongest fortress in the center of the sea of stars fall so easily?

In the blood-boiling battle cries, the golden phalanx suddenly split apart. Some of the elites of the imperial guards rushed to the sky in short-distance assault starships to support the defense line at the periphery of the space zone, while some of the elites of the imperial guards stationed themselves in

the permanent fortresses on and below the ground, preparing to fight desperately when the enemy broke into the atmosphere and burnt the entire Empyrean Terminus Planet to the ground.

Expressionlessly, Li Jialing raised his arm and saluted the soldiers with the most honorable military salute of the imperium. It was not until the last imperial guard disappeared from his vision that he retracted his stiff arm and returned to the command center of the imperial city.

Right now, the command center of the new imperium was even more chaotic.

The intelligence from the sky and the underground, the preparation and assembly of the other planets outside of Empyreal Terminus, and the news from the rebels in the east and even the Star Glory Federation were all flooding into the thousands of super crystal processors in the command center like

hundreds of rapid rivers. Even though the crystal processors boasted the best computational ability in the entire imperium, they were still squeaking as if they could collapse at any moment.

The communication soldiers and the analysts of the commander-in-chief’s department were too busy to notice the arrival of ‘His Majesty’. Everybody’s eyes were occupied by the dense main fleet of the Covenant Alliance.

Li Linghai, on the other hand, noticed the appearance of his son.

Of course, even though she was the Empress Dowager of the Imperium of True Human Beings, she still had to call her son ‘Your Majesty’ respectfully.

Besides, Li Linghai felt from the bottom of his heart that Li Jialing had indeed grown a lot in only one month. He seemed to have been driven into a corner, but the enthronement ceremony was a change. The news that Li Yao was missing was a change, too. After the two changes, he seemed to have been

reborn. He was more and more like a real, distinguished emperor. Or rather, he was more and more like Black Star Emperor, if not Wu Yingqi.

“His Majesty spoke very well just now. Not only the elites of the imperial guards gathered in front of the imperial city, all the soldiers of the imperial guards and the Immortal Cultivators of the Empyreal Terminus Sector were inspired by His Majesty, too. Their morale was soaring.

Li Linghai smiled and said, “If we win the battle, Your Majesty’s prestige will reach an unprecedented level. It is very likely that you will make a name for yourself that is far more distinguished than the Black Star Emperor!”

“Only if we can win.”

LiJialing took a long breath in relief. The golden flames around him suddenly dimmed. Even his clear and determined eyes were blurred. He coughed and said in a low voice, “What’s the situation on the eastern front exactly? Is there any solid news about the Marquis of Liaohai? When will the last mobile

troops of the fake marquis be taken care of? When will all the remaining forces of the four families be locked down and sent back?”

“Not yet.

Li Linghai said, “The situation on the eastern front is still the same. Lei Chenhu and Bai Xingjian set up an ambush with the Jade Cauldron Sector as a trap and attacked the reinforcements with the strategy of ‘surrounding the enemy’. They took down quite a few reinforcements of the four major families

and seriously wounded the morale of the enemy. It is safe to say that the fleets of the four major families have all perished. It will be difficult for them to gather a large troop and fight against us face to face.

“But that was exactly the problem.

“As far as our army is concerned, occupying the enemy’s planet is not our goal—the territory of the four families is so large that even if we open the gate for our army to occupy, how much territory can we occupy?

“Destroying the enemy’s strength, especially the mobile forces that can perform large-scale space jumps at will, is the key.

“But right now, the last joint fleet of the four major families is still hiding in the ‘Yellow Dragon Sector’, the capital city of the Song family. No matter how critical the situation of the Jade Cauldron Sector was, they never showed up and simply watched the Jade Cauldron Sector fall. It seems that the enemy

is determined to defend it to the end.

“If so, our rebels will be caught in an awkward dilemma.

“As the capital city of the Song family, which has been established for hundreds of years, the defense of the Yellow Dragon Sector is equally impregnable. Also, the last joint fleet of the four major families is stationed there. If the rebels attack the place by force, they will suffer tremendous losses.

“However, if we leave the Yellow Dragon Sector alone and continue to attack other resource-rich areas, it is very likely that the coalition fleet will break out and cut off the path of retreat for the rebels. The rebels will be alone, and it will be difficult to get effective supplies and rest. Once the path of retreat

is cut off, everything will be over.

“Therefore… Lei Chenghu and Bai Xinghe both need time to solve the tricky problem.

“Time. Time.”

Li Jialing closed his eyes and mumbled to himself. Then he asked, “What about the Star Glory Federation? Any updates?”

“Nothing.

Li Linghai said, “Think about it from another point of view. If you are the Speaker of Parliament of Cultivators, can you make a decision to march forward in a month based on the disparity in capability, the hatred between Cultivators and Immortal Cultivators, and the chaotic parliament?

“Understood. It’s a matter of time again. It seems that the people of the Star Glory Federation have calculated the timing of the invasion to the most precise degree. They have calculated that we will never receive any help!”

The corner of Li Jialing’s mouth twitched. He lowered his voice, as if he were talking to himself. “If all the soldiers and civilians in the capital city know that I’m lying to them, we will still be engaged in a two-pronged battle, surrounded by enemies and helpless… I don’t know how angry and desperate they

will be. I’m afraid that the morale of the people will collapse in a moment!

“I won’t.”

Li Linghai said casually, “Even if reinforcements are coming, it won’t be overnight. When the situation gets serious, the entire Empyreal Terminus will be bunt to the ground. Most of the people will be dead, and the survivors will be fighting for their survival. Who will remember the… white lie? At that

time, people will be more than willing for you to continue lying and give them hope that does not exist!”

LiJialing sniffed and stared at Li Linghai.

Li Linghai accepted his anger without the least bit of shame.

Right then, the latest report of the rebel army from the Jade Cauldron Sector was sent to the command center.

The battle report marked ‘top secret’ was like a million-ton crystal bomb that exploded deep inside the eyes of the emperor and the queen mother, blowing away the last bit of blood in their eyes and their faces..

Chapter 2905: Return Fleet

While the emperor and the queen mother of the new empire were deeply shocked by the earth-shattering news and could barely control the nerves on their faces and the trembling of their hands, in the Yellow Dragon Sector, the capital city of the Song family far away from the capital city, which was also

known as the so-called ‘Old Empire’, in the orbit of the main planet, inside a magnificent starship that looked like a saber that had been unsheathed, some people were sighing, frustrated by the complicated, dark situation in front of them.

It was ‘Song Bugui’, second in command of the imperium and the supreme commander of the coalition fleet, the last mobile force of the families of the four electors.

After the two consecutive defeats in the Seven Seas Space Zone and the battle of the imperial capital, the situation that they had been so confident in had collapsed instantly. Not only had they lost half of their territory, they had also suffered a crushing defeat. Their victory had turned into a perilous one.

The four electors finally showed their sincerity. They all agreed that, on this day when the ‘King Kong Fleet’ and the ‘Royal Card Fleet’ had both suffered a crushing defeat and even turned against each other, the remnants of their forces, which were weak and scattered in their lone island-like territory,

would only be destroyed one by one by the unstoppable revolutionaries.

The only way out was to gather the few newly-arrived warships and defend the dignity of His Majesty and the four electors.

Therefore, the coalition fleet was born.

From the name of the fleet that was granted by the emperor, ‘Returning to Heaven’, it was evident how important the last fleet was to the entire ‘Old Empire’.

However, Song Bugui, the supreme commander of the ‘Returning Sky Fleet’, was not the commander-in-chief that everybody expected. Even he was well aware that he was merely making do with the compromise and helplessness of the power struggle.

Among the shining stars of the imperial army, Song Bugui, who was 184 years old, was not one of the most brilliant generals, nor did he have anything to do with the adjectives such as bravery and resourcefulness. In his 150-year military career, he had experienced dozens of bloody battles, big and small,

but no matter how relaxed the conditions were, he could not find any classic examples that could be included in the textbooks, much less a splendid record of ‘surprise attack from thousands of miles away’.

He was just an ordinary general who didn’t have the slightest bit of personal style or the temperament of a famous general. He was more like a step-by-step civil servant than a general who roamed the battlefield. In the first half of his military career, he would often show up as a staff officer or a deputy.

‘When he was qualified enough to take charge of his own affairs, he would often go to defend the desolate, barren world or be responsible for logistics and escort missions. He had once spent more than ten years idling in the military command department and as the education director of the imperial fleet.

For some reason, he was promoted to the position of commander-in-chief of a certain Front Army just so that he could take the blame that nobody else was willing to.

In the dozens of bloody battles and fierce battles, his performance could only be said to be passable. He had been defeated in battles before, but he had not been completely defeated. In the worst circumstances, he had been able to safely evacuate with 70% to 80% of the remaining soldiers. He had won

victories, but because of his hesitation, he had missed the opportunity. He had let go of a good opportunity to expand his achievements and get promoted.

Generally speaking, he was an inconspicuous general that no one would expect. But when it came down to it, he did not have many shortcomings.

As an Immortal Cultivator, Song Bugui might have been ambitious when he was young, but after more than a hundred years of hard work, blood, and tears, and dozens of times when he was on the brink of death, the old Song Bugui was well aware that he was just a mediocre man who had no ability to

realize his ambitions.

Before the four major families launched their attack on the Seven Seas Space Zone, he had been kicked back to the cold bench of the director of education of the Star Ocean Imperium Fleet and the reserve commander of the Song family. At that time, he had been so frustrated that he had been waiting to

sit on the cold bench until his death, But he had never expected that he would become the commander of the last elite troop of the four major families and one of the top ‘big shots’ in the imperial army after only half a year.

Of course, Song Bugui was self-aware enough to know what was going on—he was surrendering and dying, taking away his talented and brilliant companions one by one. In the Seven Seas Space Zone, Yun Xuefeng and Song Yushi, as well as countless real-life and well-connected commanders of the four

royal fleets, had either been killed in battle or ended up in the hands of the revolutionaries, with an extremely dishonorable ending.

Just like that, the leadership of the four families, who were running away, finally managed to stabilize themselves. When they looked around in confusion, they were surprised to find that they could not pick a fleet commander who had both prestige and expertise!

Now that the main force of the four families had been defeated and they were in desperate need of reserves and new soldiers to fill the fleet, Song Bugui, who had been the director of education of the Star Ocean Imperium and the commander of the Song family’s reserves all year round, was the only

candidate that everybody approved of.

Just like that, Song Bugui was appointed as the supreme commander of the Heaven’s Return Fleet in a muddle-headed manner. He was even bestowed with an out-of-place ‘Sub-Marshal’ rank—this was a new rank established after the new emperor ascended the throne. Originally, there was only a

difference between a general and a marshal in the empire. For the supreme commander of the coalition fleet of the four major families, it was never too much to award him the rank of ‘Marshal’, just like Lei Chenghu, who was ‘on the other side’. But perhaps even Song Bugui himself felt that awarding him

the rank of ‘Marshal’ was too absurd, too outrageous, and too unworthy of the title of ‘Marshal of the Empire’ over the past few hundred years. So, if he was granted the title of ‘Sub-Marshal’, then he would be granted the title of ‘Sub-Marshal’!

At least, Song Bugui looked at the title on his shoulder and thought that it was as appropriate as his awkward situation.

Although he had become the supreme commander of the Heaven’s Return Fleet, the “military big shot” in theory, Song Bugui didn’t have the slightest bit of a sense of monopoly. He was very clear that he, as the supreme commander, was nothing more than a mascot on the surface. At most, he had the

authority to command and give suggestions. However, the Heaven’s Return Fleet wasn’t a united fleet at all. Its control was still firmly in the hands of the big shots of the various families. Without the approval of the Elected Emperor, he couldn’t even command the Song Family’s own fleet.

Not only was his control over his subordinates extremely weak, there were also dozens of ‘mother-in-law’ above his head. As the saying goes, even a dog cannot change its habit of eating sh*t. It was even more impossible for the noble families of the four major families to not fight for power and profit

than it was for a dog to not eat sh*t. Even in the early stages of the great collapse, the four families were still able to unite sincerely and establish a fleet to return to the heavens. However, after they gained a bit of a foothold, they inevitably revealed their selfish nature. Especially after the new emperor

ascended to the throne as Emperor Wu, those who elected meritorious ‘subordinates’ did not even try to conceal their desire for power and ambitions. They sold off their titles and positions, wantonly bestowed rewards, and seized the opportunity to rise in power through open and secret conflicts. This

sort of thing was not unusual. It happened endlessly and was getting more and more intense!

It did make sense now that he thought about it. The imperial court under the control of the four major families had collapsed to such an extent that even the imperial capital had been lost. The only way to gather more people was to reward them generously.

In the meantime, after the collapse of the King Kong Fleet and the Royal Card Fleet, countless generals and lords had perished, leaving a lot of empty seats. There was no time to lose now.

In the beginning, Song Bugui didn’t care about these dirty, dirty, and messy things. Just the thought of him facing the so-called ‘God of War’, the Marquis of the Ocean’s Distant Sea, was enough to make people shiver and have their eyes turn black!

More than fifty years ago, Song Bugui had worked with Lei Chenghu for a while. At that time, he knew exactly what a famous general was and the gap between him and a real famous general.

Song Bugui estimated that he only had two options. He could either be crushed by Lei Chenghu on the battlefield as if he were chopping vegetables, and his flagship would be blown to smithereens along with him, or he could be defeated and return to Traveller with his face covered in dust. He could

obediently take the blame and accept the wrath and punishment of Emperor Wu.

If it were not so dangerous, why was the ‘commander-in-chief of the fleet’ stuck to his ass and could not be shaken off?

Song Bugui didn’t want to care about the rotten affairs of the imperial court.

However, the new king, or rather, the four electors, had expanded the bureaucracy of the entire imperial court and the military in order to win the support of the people. There was also the military government outside the military command department. Above the military government, there was the

General Staff Department. Next to the General Staff Department, there was the Commanding Department. All the messy structures were ‘managed’ by a base camp. Above the base camp, a damn military committee was established. Dozens of ministers, chiefs, vice chiefs, and heads of the committee were

all allowed to intervene in the preparation and command of the Heaven’s Return Fleet. Countless ‘wonderful plans’ were tossed out by all of them, the kind that could crush ‘God of War’ Lei Chenghu without any trouble. All of them stared at Song Bugui warily and almost poked his nose, berating him for

his fear of the enemy and even conspiring with the rebels.

If he hadn’t secretly colluded with the rebels, how could he, as the commander who controlled the strongest forces of the four major families, still bide his time and hesitate to move forward when the rebellious fleets were plundering the Song family’s territory, flaunting their might, and even destroying

several reinforcement fleets and supply fleets?

As for general chiefs, sub chiefs, and ministers, Song Bugui could deal with them easily.

However, faced with the suspicion of Song Lixing, the Chairman of the Supreme Military Council of the Imperium of True Human Beings, Song Bugui could not do it half-heartedly.

It was not just because Song Lixing was his immediate superior in the military system, nor was it because Song Lixing had made great contributions in the process of promoting the new emperor to the throne. It was also because Song Lixing was the eldest son of the head of the Song family, one of the

four electors, and the most promising heir of the next emperor and the head of the Song family.. Naturally, Song Bugui had been taken advantage of in all aspects!

Chapter 2906: Military And Political

“Why are we still not attacking?”

On the 3D light beam, Song Lixing was staring at Song Bugui with a gloomy face. His eyes, which were full of thorns and thorns, seemed to be piercing all the way from the surface of the planet to the geosynchronous orbit. He whipped Song Bugui’s spine and said, “Deputy General Song, His Majesty and

my father are both very concerned. The revolutionary army has marched into the heartland of the Song family for such a long time and even occupied the ‘Jade Cauldron Sector’, our most important resource and heavy industry base. They are plundering and savaging everything. The entire Jade Cauldron

Sector is about to be destroyed!

“At this critical moment of life and death, with the strongest fleet of the imperium in your hands, why are you not reinforcing the Jade Tripod Sector and fighting against the revolutionary army? What are you waiting for? What are you hesitating for? What are you scared of?”

Song Lixing’s face was handsome, and his voice was as loud as a bell. Even when he was blowing his beard and glaring, there was a hint of loyalty in his voice.

However, Song Bugui was very clear that the future chief of the clan was an out-and-out idiot who had nothing but gold and jade on the outside. He was even a shameless villain who would send himself to his death if he received any credit.

He was the kind of person who considered himself to be a chess player and others to be pigs. He believed that the entire world revolved around him.

Unfortunately, because of his strong background, his idea was not wrong most of the time.

He did not have any military talents. The few battles that he commanded in person were all idiotic. Other than keeping his head low and marching forward like a pig, he did not have any other tactics. Instead, he was promoting the ideology of ‘contribution, sacrifice, long live His Majesty’. Besides, he had

the gift of self-taught skills, such as seizing credit, eliminating dissidents, shirking responsibility, and sabotaging his subordinates and comrades.

He had relied on the blood and corpses of countless people, as well as the glory of his father, to build up his own path of advancement step by step. Song Bugui had personally seen his companions being sent into the fire pit by him several times. He had even nearly been shot out like a cannonball, dying

without even knowing how.

It was funny that this guy thought that everyone in the world was blind and didn’t know or had long forgotten his misdeeds. He often bragged that he was the one who promoted Song Bugui to the position of the supreme commander of the Heaven’s Return Fleet and the vice commander of the Star Ocean

Imperium. He wanted Song Bugui to be grateful to him day and night!

Although his heart was filled with frustration and disdain, Song Bugui still maintained a cautious and slow appearance on the surface. He said obsequiously, “Chairman, the Heaven’s Return Fleet is still in the middle of rehearsals. You should know that, although our fleet is equipped with a large number

of newly-arrived warships, they belong to the four families… The remnants of the army and the reserves have been assembled. The cooperation between the four families has not been polished to the point of perfection. The morale of the officers and the soldiers is not very high, either. On the contrary,

the reformation of the rebel army is the opposite. After winning two battles in a row, they will charge into the hinterland of our fleet like a hot knife cutting through butter. They will be unstoppable. This is the time when the morale of the army is soaring and unstoppable.

“In my opinion, since the enemy’s morale is at its peak, we might as well avoid them. There’s no need to start a final battle rashly. Let’s defend the Yellow Dragon Sector with the fleet of the Heaven’s Origin Sector and the reserves that have arrived from various places. We can even have a final battle with

the enemy in the Yellow Dragon Sector. The Yellow Dragon Sector is the core location that our Song family has painstakingly built for hundreds of years. It definitely won’t be taken over so easily. If the revolutionary army really dares to come, the Yellow Dragon Sector will become a slaughterhouse for

them!”

His words seemed to be bold, but in fact, they were extremely prudent. Song Lixing was not satisfied at all. The eyes of the future head of the Song family were wide open. He observed Song Bugui from head to toe for a long time before he said, “What are you talking about? The revolutionary bandit army

is unstoppable—isn’t that still your problem? Why didn’t you intercept and annihilate the enemy when they just entered the Song family’s territory?

“Right now, you are so scared of the enemy that you don’t even dare to go out and fight them?

“Hehe. Stretching out our stance in the Yellow Dragon Sector and waiting for the revolutionary army to attack? What a great idea! Let me ask you, what about the Jade Tripod Sector? Are we going to abandon the most precious resources, heavy industry bases, and shipyards of the Song family just like

that? Also, the Yellow Dragon Sector is where the Son of Heaven is patrolling right now. His Majesty’s palace is right here. Are you going to stand by and watch the rebel army conquer the Yellow Dragon Sector, expand their strength crazily with the resources of the Yellow Dragon Sector, and march to His

Majesty and all the officials of the imperial court? What is this? Is this how the Song family defends His Majesty and the imperial court?”

Song Bugui wanted to say that, when Lei Chenghu and Bai Xinghe marched into the heartland of the Song family, he had only just become the unfortunate commander of the Heaven’s Retum Fleet. It was exactly because no one from the four major families dared to fight against Lei Chenghu that he, the

unlucky bastard, and the scapegoat had to make up the numbers. It was already a miracle that he had trained the remnants of the four major families and a bunch of reserves who were barely qualified to be called ‘elites’ in such a short period of time. How come Lei Chenghu was trying to blame it on him?

He took a deep breath and reminded himself not to be angry. Over the past few days, he had been thinking hard about how to deal with Lei Chenghu. His blood, sweat, and tears had been drained, and his soul was extremely weak. If he were to burst into fury again, it was very likely that he would become

mentally deranged. He explained patiently, “Chairman, the Jade Cauldron Sector won’t fall so easily. Right now, the garrison fleet of the Jade Cauldron Sector has been swept clean, but the Jade Cauldron Planet and a few resource planets are still in the hands of the ground guards. As a heavy industry base,

the Jade Cauldron Planet also has a very complete defense system on the ground and underground. As long as the Song army doesn’t act rashly, it won’t be a problem for them to hold on for three to five years.

“On the other hand, the Heaven’s Return Fleet is not necessarily a revolutionary force on the battlefield.

“Are you trying to say that we are no match for the revolutionary army?”

Song Lixing narrowed his eyes and asked coldly.

“No. No. What I meant was that the threat might be greater when the saber is not unsheathed. There is no need for the fleet of the Heaven’s Origin Sector to fight the revolutionary army head-on. As long as they lurk in the Yellow Dragon Sector and do nothing, they will be the greatest deterrence for the

revolutionary army!

Song Bugui said, “With the Anti-Heaven Fleet eyeing us covetously in the darkness, it’s impossible for the revolutionary army to devote 100% of their forces to attack the surface of the Jade Tripod Planet and a few resource satellites. If that’s the case, we will be at each other’s throats, and the defense line

will be foolproof.”

“The revolutionary army seems to have the advantage in the battlefield, but their disadvantage is obvious, too, which is that they are marching alone and lack supplies. They cannot turn their advantage into victory. As long as they attack for a long time, their morale will definitely be low, and there will be

a riot in the rear. At that time, our army will give them a thunderous strike, and they will definitely fall!”

“Hehe…”

Song Lixing’s face was still gloomy. He did not comment on Song Bugui’s proposal.

“Chairman, I think this is the safest approach in the military.

Song Bugui made up his mind. He braced himself and said, “It’s not that I’m scared of the enemy. It’s just that Lei Chenghu is far better than me in strategy, tactics, and prestige. It’s also a fact. Why should we attack recklessly when we have the opportunity to?”

“Deputy Marshal Song, you mean to say that you know military, but I don’t?”

Song Lixing’s eyes were even wider than before, but his nostrils were even wider than his eyes. He said coldly, “We both know military affairs, but I know one thing more than you—politics!

“I don’t care what the military thinks of the current situation, but from a political point of view, the Jade Cauldron Sector must be retaken, and the flames of war must not reach the Yellow Dragon Sector!

“Firstly, the Jade Tripod Sector is the most important resource and heavy industry base for the Song family. It is the biggest capital that the Song family relies on to conquer the sea of stars. We cannot stand by and watch the flames of war sweep to the surface and underground of the Jade Tripod Planet

and burn our biggest capital! That’s what my father meant. That’s what I meant, too. I think, as a member of the Song family, you should have the same thought, right?

“Secondly, there are a lot of rumors in the imperial court that the revolutionary rebels are marching unstoppably and threatening His Majesty’s safety. Therefore, the emperor’s palace has to be moved from the Song family to the territories of the other families. Humph. Those short-sighted pigs and dogs

are trying to play the trick of ‘the narrow emperor commands the feudal vassals’. Do you think that we can give it up so easily after we finally found a new emperor with the help of the Yellow Dragon Sector?

“Well…”

Song Bugui’s eyes bulged. He did not expect, or rather, did not have the time to think about it.

Obviously, in the territory of the emperor, whoever was the overlord of the four electors would be in charge of the imperial court.

However, it did not mean that the other three sects were no match for the new dominator.

Right now, the Song family was the leader of the four major families. Naturally, it was the target of all the other three families.

Song Bugui, on the other hand, didn’t think so. At this critical moment, the other three families were really trying to get their hands on the hot potato that was Emperor Chengwu. It was quite clear that whoever had the Emperor would be able to reform the rebels and beat them up. Other than the Song

Family, who else would be able to withstand Lei Chenghu’s wrath?

However, under the pretext of ‘suggesting a mobile palace for His Majesty’s safety’, it was quite normal for the nobles to exploit the Song family for more benefits.

“My father has been under tremendous pressure in the imperial court. He has promised His Majesty and all the civil and military officials that he will not allow the revolutionary rebels to invade any space zone of the Yellow Dragon Sector and annihilate the enemy in the Jade Tripod Sector!

Song Lixing said resolutely, “Deputy General Song, what do you think?”

Song Bugui opened his mouth, only to be filled with confusion and speechless.

“No”

Seeing that he was caught between a rock and a hard place, Song Lixing changed his tone and said in a low voice, “We are all members of the Song family. Let me tell you the truth. According to the imperial court, we are definitely going to fight, but there are a lot of rules on how to fight.. Do you want to

hear them?”

Chapter 2907: Weird Internal Conflict

Song Bugui could only say, “Please elaborate.”

“The fleet of the Heaven’s Origin Sector is not a united team right now. The forces of the four parties are still minding their own business. As the commander-in-chief, you should be very clear about that, shouldn’t you?

Song Lixing said, “What’s even more hateful is that those pigs and dogs of the Yun family, the Li family, and the Dongfang family can’t stand the fact that the Song family holds the military power of the Heaven’s Return Fleet. There are a lot of criticisms in the court. Father and I have helped you block a

lot of ambushes!

“But right now, the selfish, disloyal guys failed to take back the command of the fleet and proposed to revoke the establishment of the Heaven’s Return Fleet. The four fleets should split up and go home to defend their nests in case their rear is ambushed.

“Well—”

“Go now, Juncai. Believe in your father for once. This might be the only way to save your family.”
