The redness in Song Bugui’s eyes seemed to be burning his eldest son. The eldest son felt that his eyes and brain were burning, too, as if a volcano was erupting. He saluted Song Bugui and left in a hurry.

Watching his eldest son gradually disappear into the darkness of the pathway, Song Bugui sat back in the commander-in-chief’s seat. He licked the blood that had splashed out when he smashed the control panel and summoned the advisor and other members of the bridge team while he pondered

emotionlessly.

Before the bridge crew and the leadership of the fleet arrived, the seasoned general of the imperium, the commander-in-chief of the fleet of the Heaven’s Origin Sector, and the remaining strategical Immortal Cultivators of the coalition army of the four electors smiled bitterly and mumbled to themselves

ina low voice.

“The imperium… is doomed!”

Chapter 2910: A Tiger In The Mountain!

To be fair, the noble families of the coalition army were not all idiots who were blinded by greed. Bai Xinghe’s mutiny and Lei Chenghu’s chaos were too abrupt, and it was easy for people to wonder whether or not a dangerous trap had been set up in the dark and obscure universe of the Jade Tripod Sector.

But since ancient times, the effectiveness of traps had little to do with the sophistication of the traps themselves. More importantly, the bait placed inside the traps was not attractive enough, and the animals that walked in front of the traps were not hungry enough.

People often only believed what they were willing to believe. A wild beast that had gone mad from hunger would only have bright meat in its eyes, and its nose would only be able to smell the fragrance of fat. It would simply be unable to see the bright edge of a blade ina trap.

Right now, the coalition army of the electors was such a hungry, reckless beast.

As the son of an elected emperor, Song Lixing’s strategic insight and wariness were as good as Song Bugui’s. But his and Song Bugui’s sincere words were not wrong at all. It was not a military issue but a political one whether or not to attack the Jade Tripod Sector.

The revolutionaries were having a hard time, and so were the four electors. After two crushing defeats, more than seventy percent of their best warships and well-trained elites had been lost. The consequences were not limited to the battlefield. A series of disastrous consequences were gradually

surfacing.

Every day, countless demonstrations and illegal gatherings would break out in the territory of the four families.

Every day, countless rumors would spread out in the form of flyers or secret channels, promoting the advocates of the revolutionaries, “Enchanting the ignorant people”.

Every day, countless middle- and low-level nobles would secretly contact the capital city and receive the promise of the capital city. As long as they responded to the “reformation”, not only would their crimes be forgiven, they would also be able to take a share of the fat corpses of the nobles.

Such demonstrations were full of plots and betrayals. It was impossible to eliminate all of them by force.

Besides, the four electors were not strong enough to suppress the riot at all.

After all, in an empire where the strong preyed on the weak and the winners reigned supreme, losing multiple battles in a head-on battle would mean that you had lost all your prestige and the power to intimidate and suppress. Who would listen to you?

‘There were also resources. Simply speaking, money.

Song Bugui, as the commander of the fleet, could not care less about the military expenses of the Flying Star Fleet, the maintenance of the palace, or the cost to stabilize the morale of the people. However, the four electors had to go through all the trouble for a copper coin. Since the imperial capital fell

into the hands of the revolutionaries, the credit of the ‘empire crystal coins’ issued by the imperial court had plummeted. Not to mention the outside world, even the few Core Worlds under the rule of the four families were not very useful. There were even absurd situations where people would rather

trade with ‘free star coins’ or use several special crystals as hard currency than use the imperial crystal coins.

Violence could take away the lives of those who resisted.

But it was impossible to suppress the resistance of the capital with force.

After all, when the executor of the armed forces, the soldiers and the middle-level officers, were not willing to accept the crystal coins of the Star Ocean Imperium as their salary, even the strongest armed forces would collapse and vanish.

Right now, with the abundant assets that the four families had accumulated over the past hundreds of years, they could still support the last bit of credit of the crystal coins of the Star Ocean Imperium. They could even resort to the primitive methods of ‘military tickets’, ‘national bonds’, and ‘barter’. But

everybody knew how inefficient and unsustainable such trading methods were in an information society that spanned billions of lightyears.

It did not take many professional financial experts to estimate that the four electors were all very clear that, without a satisfactory victory to boost morale and raise the public’s confidence in the crystal coins of the imperium, the economic foundation of the ‘orthodox government of the Imperium of True

Human Beings’ would be demolished.

This was the truth that Song Lixing had spoken when he was exasperated—Without money, who the hell would know whether you were the Elector of Empires or Emperor Chengwu?

Other than that, the most critical and fatal point was that the coalition army of the candidates did not have a clear understanding about the scale and combat ability of the main fleet of the Covenant Alliance.

It was only natural.

Since the very beginning, the ‘Parliament of the Imperium’, which was controlled by the four electors, had been insisting that the counterattack of the imperium over the past twenty years was a great victory and that the main force of the Covenant Alliance had been crushed. The people of the Covenant Alliance

Alliance had been driven back to their barren hometown, and they could cause no more trouble.

The ministers in the temple all scoffed at Lei Chenghu’s argument that the main force of the Covenant Alliance did not suffer too many losses and was still looking for opportunities in the darkness.

Although such claims were partly self-aggrandizing, after twenty years of bragging, many nobles had been too immersed in the victory lies to stop themselves from believing them.

There were no omniscient or omnipotent deities in the camp of the four electors. How could they have known that the main force of the Star Glory Federation was not only intact but also controlled by a super crystal processor named ‘Fuxi’, which had been passed on since the primeval era and boasted the

greatest computational ability in the entire Pangu Universe?

Therefore, in the evaluation of the four electors, the overall scale and combat ability of the invasion fleet of the Covenant Alliance were at least 70% lower than the real number.

By inputting the shrunken original data into the crystal processor and deducing the strategy, a ‘terrifying’ conclusion had been reached.

The assault of the members of the Star Glory Federation was nothing more than harassment. At best, it was revenge out of fury, revenge against the imperial capital because they were not willing to let dozens of Sectors be taken away for nothing.

Or rather, the members of the Star Glory Federation were merely bluffing, They were attacking the capital city on the surface, but their target was still the Sectors that they had just lost.

In other words, it was possible for the ‘reformed rebels’ to defend the Empyrean Terminus Sector and Empyrean Terminus.

This was quite bad.

No matter how much the noble families denied it on the surface, the truth was that the revolutionaries had enchanted the hearts of most of the citizens of the imperium and the low-level Immortal Cultivators. Now that they had a certain ‘righteousness’ in their hands, they were becoming more and more

the ‘wish of the people’.

The battles on the candidate’s side had been a total mess. It was quite disappointing. If the revolutionaries defeated the intruders of the Covenant Alliance and secured the Empyrean Terminus Sector and the Empyrean Terminus Sector, wouldn’t the identity of ‘True Destiny’s Child’ be confirmed?

As time went by, the people’s will and the public’s will gathered toward the revolutionaries. If so, the candidate for the throne would truly die a graveless death.

Therefore, the war had to be fought and won. The revolutionary rebels had to be divided into two sides and annihilated when Lei Chenghu lost one of his arms in order to regain the initiative.

Was this some sort of ‘victory belief?

Over the next twelve hours, more precise intelligence was sent to the palace of the Yellow Dragon Sector.

According to reliable sources, Lei Chenghu and Bai Xinghe, the two ‘thief generals’, had been having conflicts over the past half month.

The biggest contradiction was whether or not the troops should take a rest.

Although the reformation of the rebels’ attack on the Jade Cauldron Sector seemed smooth and did not encounter much resistance, it was still a small fleet that was attacking the enormous planet after all. Besides, the continuous ‘surroundings’ had consumed a lot of fuel, ammunition, and soldiers.

Although they did not capture Jade Tripod, they had captured a lot of starships and assets inside the star fortress.

Bai Xinghe meant that the troops should stop attacking for three days and digest the resources inside the starships and the fortress. Otherwise, the battle would be too hard.

Lei Chenghu, on the other hand, turned down his proposal brutally and even scolded him mercilessly for the bandit’s style. The suppression on Jade Cauldron Planet could not be stopped for even a second until the defenders surrendered. If the defenders were given a chance to catch their breath, all their

previous efforts would have been in vain.

Their opinions were not wrong, but there was a subtle difference in their stances. Lei Chenghu was dedicated to the new imperium and would not hesitate to fight until there were no survivors, but Bai Xinghe was more selfish and considered the fleet under his command his own property. How could he

allow his capital to be wasted in the civil war of the imperium?

It was the starting point of their disagreement. Later, the war became more and more intense, and the gap between them grew deeper and deeper. When the Star Glory Federation sent out the message that they were not going to send out their troops, it was the straw that broke the camel’s back. Bai

Xinghe had indeed demonstrated the sordid and shameless style of the bandits to the best of his ability. While Lei Chenghu’s subordinates were attacking the No. 1 satellite of the Jade Cauldron Planet, he had retreated at full speed without caring about anything else and exposed Lei Chenghu’s flank. When

he retreated, he had taken with him most of the trophies that he had retrieved from the previous battlefield and the starships that were almost intact.

‘The commander-in-chief of the garrison of Jade Cauldron Planet was a man who knew his soldiers well. He took the opportunity to charge out of the atmosphere and attack Lei Chenghu’s weak flank, dragging the fleet of the ‘god of war’ into a muddy melee. The chaotic battle was still going on even at

this moment. No matter how brilliant the art of command was, it could not be performed. It was all based on morale, courage, and valor. In that regard, the revolutionary army, which had received bad news one after another, was definitely not at an advantage.

Of course, most of the main warships of the defenders of the Jade Cauldron Sector had been wrecked or taken away by Bai Xingjian. The few destroyers that were left were enough for them to maneuver in the low-Earth orbit. After such a long time, Lei Chenghu would be able to get rid of the chaos, clean

up the mess, and devour the defenders.

Therefore, the defenders of the Jade Cauldron Sector were in desperate need of the reinforcements of the Yellow Dragon Sector to finish the battle!

The unquestionable intelligence ignited the fire of war in the hearts of all the nobles.

Cowardice and arrogance were two sides of the same coin. The timidest of hearts were often burnt by the illusory flames into an arrogant and fearless glow—although the glow could only last for a short moment.

Most of the nobles had been avoiding Lei Chenghu as if he were a devil or a scorpion the day before. Right now, they were jumping up and down, rubbing their fists, eager to crush the ‘God of War’ on Lei Chenghu’s head..

Chapter 2911: Moving Towards Tiger Ridge!

“The ‘God of War’ of the past is too old now. He was fooled by a lieutenant who was merely a space pirate and lost an arm for nothing, What’s there to be scared of?

“The rebels are already caught in chaos. As long as the Heavenly Soldiers arrive and launch a thunderous attack, the rebels will certainly be blown to smithereens!

“We annihilated the rebels in the Jade Cauldron Sector, and the rebels of the Empyrean Terminus Sector suffered heavy losses against the members of the Star Glory Federation. The final victory was secured without any trouble!

It could be said that he was blowing his own trumpet, that he was trying to cheer himself up, that he was trying to numb his nerves, or that he was forcing himself to have the belief that he was going to win. In short, in less than half a day, such arguments were already flying everywhere inside and

outside the palace on Yellow Dragon.

Below the four electors, almost all the nobles were eager to fight. None of them were as prudent as Song Bugui.

There was something else that was not worth mentioning. A lot of starships and soldiers in the rebel fleet commanded by Lei Chenghu were captured by the revolutionaries in the Battle of the Seven Seas and the battle of the imperial capital.

In other words, the starships had been painstakingly built by the four families over hundreds of years!

The starships had only joined the revolutionaries not long ago. It was impossible to purge all the officers and soldiers who were in charge of the starships. There were still a lot of personnel left in the old system. Many of them might have found that the situation was not good and decided to serve their

old masters again.

At least, that was what the four electors thought.

Therefore, of course, they had to win first place and try to recruit more starships under their command from Lei Chenghu’s side. It would be great if they could capture other starships, too.

Driven by the complicated and unpredictable mentality, the fleets stationed in the Yellow Dragon Sector were all ready for battle.

Not just the main force of the fleet, even the few defense fleets in low-Earth orbit that were not suitable for large-scale space jumps, and even the supplementary fleets made of semi-armed carriers, were packed with the ambitious Immortal Cultivators who were trying to take advantage of the situation

and shout that they would “defend the honor of His Majesty’.

In the hinterland of the other few sects, a lot of starships that had been gathered in a hurry requested the coordinates of the Jade Tripod Sector so that they could jump there and fight together.

The starships were truly a bunch of mobs. Usually, they would flee when they heard Lei Chenghu’s name. But right now, they were trying to make up the numbers.

Faced with the chaotic, noisy, and self-serving situation, the entire Song family was having a headache, but there was nothing they could do.

Song Lixing, on the other hand, was more and more in favor of Song Bugui’s suggestion. It seemed that he had to find a way to gather all the big shots of the military and monitor them closely. Otherwise, who knew whether or not they would bypass Song Bugui and directly issue an order to their

respective fleets to fight and take the credit?

Song Bugui’s words had already burnt away Song Lixing’s rationality. All he could think of was how to defeat Lei Chenhu and become the next ‘god of war.

Of course, Song Lixing would never force the leaders of the military to board his flagship. It would be too obvious and foolish.

Song Lixing knew exactly what those people were thinking. He merely announced that he would board the flagship with part of the leadership of the military committee and challenge Lei Chenghu in person as the commander-in-chief. The officers of the military command department, the military

government department, the main camp, the staff department, and the national defense committee all crowded around him like flies that had smelled sweetness. They crowded onto the flagship together with him impatiently, insisting that they should jointly command and fight against the God of War’.

It seemed that none of the nobles of the four noble families was willing to let go of the great opportunity to ‘defeat Lei Chenghu’. None of them were willing to watch Lei Chenghu’s head fall into somebody else’s hands. They would rather stick together and monitor each other. If I can’t get Lei Chenghu’s

head, you won’t, either!

When the high and mighty military leaders and nobles boarded the flagship, Song Bugui, the commander-in-chief of the fleet, had been waiting at the entrance of the starship with his head lowered.

The big shots, on the other hand, despised him and simply walked past him.

It was true that Lei Chenghu’s subordinates were in a mess right now. Victory was within their reach. After they took back their starship, it was only natural that they rebuilt their own fleet. The fleet of the Heaven’s Origin Sector was merely a deformed product of the transition. Why should it continue to

exist?

He had chosen Song Bugui, the Education Director’ of the Star Ocean Imperium, to be the commander-in-chief of the fleet because he had always been mediocre and prudent. He did not have much ambition or strength, and he was a role that all parties could rest assured that he would not utter a single

sound even if the blame hit his head.

Right now, maybe in two to three days, it would be of no use at all. Why bother with him?

Only Song Lixing and Song Bugui exchanged a meaningful look when they boarded the flagship.

“Chairman…”

‘When nobody was around, Song Bugui said in a low voice, “So many high officials have boarded our flagship, but there don’t seem to be many guards?”

“Because there are too many high-level officials who belong to various sects, many of whom have deep grudges with each other in the past. No one is willing to let other people bring too many guards. Therefore, Il take the lead and no one will bring any.”

Song Lixing explained, “Besides, the elite soldiers under your command, the Core Formation Stage Cultivators and Nascent Soul Stage Cultivators who boast amazing combat ability, have all been assigned to the arsenals and assault warships on the frontline. They are preparing to march into Lei

Chenghu’s battle formation and harvest his head. If they are lucky, they might even be able to harvest Lei Chenghu’s head directly. Which expert would be willing to let such a great achievement be stolen by somebody else?

“Isn’t it safe here? We can’t let Lei Chenhu’s starship crash into our flagship, can we?

“How is that possible?”

Song Bugui was amused. “You worry too much, Chairman.”

“That will do.”

Taking a deep breath, Song Lixing looked at the clusters of starships through the 360-degree virtual window and waved his hands. “Let’s go. Lei Chenghu’s head is definitely ours!”

The exhaust flames surged out, and the stars were shaking. The hundred thousand starships tore apart the raging tides raised by the three-dimensional space at the same time and enshrouded the entire Yellow Dragon Sector, covering the planets and stars in colorful gauze and reflecting the most

brilliant colors.

The coalition army of the starships and the nobles lunged at the Jade Tripod Sector unstoppably.

The fire of war in the Jade Cauldron Sector had been raging for more than half a month. Jade Cauldron Planet and several other resource planets had been besieged by the revolutionary rebels. The entire space zone was full of the debris of starships and the intense interference force fields. Much of the

battlefield data could not be transmitted.

It was not until the coalition army of the Heaven’s Return Fleet and the nobles jumped to the Jade Tripod Sector that they were able to scan the entire space zone and establish tactical communication links and battlefield data models to reach out to the defenders on the ground.

The Yellow Dragon Sector and the Jade Cauldron Sector were not far away from each other. It was a fairly smooth short-distance space jump. The fleet of the Heaven’s Origin Sector had even maintained a fairly intact formation. There were no such things as ‘blind jumps’ where starships had been blown

away by four-dimensional storms and were scattered in a range of tens of billions of kilometers.

The latest intelligence that they received boosted the morale of the nobles, too.

Firstly, the surface of Jade Cauldron and the few important resource planets was still under his control and had not been breached by the rebels.

It meant that they had a solid defense and supply base.

Secondly, intense space ripples were spreading out in a certain coordinate near the orbit of the fourth planet of the Jade Tripod Sector, which seemed to be the traces of large-scale space jumps.

It was the most fragile place with the most natural wormholes in the entire Jade Tripod Sector. It was also the busiest space jump point in history. It was the ‘proof’ of Bai Xinghe’s hasty escape!

Thirdly, the commander of the defense team reported in person that, after a day and a night of fierce combat, his fleet in the low-Earth orbit failed to stop Lei Chenghu’s remaining troops and even lost 90% of its combat ability.

However, Lei Chenghu did not pursue further. He destroyed the fleet of the defenders and occupied Yuding. Instead, he chose to clean up the mess and ran toward the No. 4 planet together with the remnants of his fleet.

According to the commander of the defense team, Lei Chenghu was in a hurry and did not even clean up the battlefield. He even abandoned many starships that were relatively slower and retreated from the battlefield without caring about anything.

“Lei Chenghu knows the situation is hopeless and is ready to run away!”

“He must’ve known that we were coming. His doom has come!”

“Go after him! Don’t let him get away!”

The starships of the fleet were brimming with fearless optimism.

On the flagship, the delegates of the Supreme Alliance Meeting were all celebrating the most brilliant victory in advance.

Right now, everything was good. There was only one last question.

At first, it was Bai Xinghe. Now, it was Lei Chenghu. The direction in which they fled in a hurry was the best space jump point of the Jade Cauldron Sector. The No. 4 planet was also the center of the war between the two parties over the past half month. The entire space zone was filled with intense

electromagnetic interference, debris of starships, and unexploded cosmic mines. It was like a thick mist of war. Right now, nobody could tell what was going on there. There was no telling how many soldiers Lei Chenghu had left, or the morale, damage, and combat ability of the remaining soldiers.

As the nominal commander-in-chief of the fleet, Song Bugui had done his part.

He suggested that they take it slow and steady and send out a small batch of scouts to carry a lot of scanning magical equipment and scout crystal cameras so that they could figure out what was going on around the fourth space jump point.

Such a prudent proposal was immediately attacked by the leaders of the military..

Chapter 2912: Capturing Lei Chenghu Alive!

“From the very beginning, you avoided battle and could only watch as the revolutionaries wreaked havoc. Even when they were about to arrive in front of His Majesty, you remained completely indifferent! At that time, it could still be explained as ‘cautiousness’. Right now, our army has the complete

advantage. The enemy army is on the run from defeat, yet we still have to hesitate and delay the opportunity. Vice Marshal Song, what exactly are you thinking?”

“The defenders of Jade Cauldron Planet have sent all the records of the recent battles to us. We can see them clearly from the combat data and even the video files. Bai Xinghe’s subordinates have indeed escaped Lei Chenghu’s control and disappeared into the depths of the sea of stars through space jumps.

The morale of the remaining Lei Chenghu’s subordinates has hit rock bottom, too. They suffered tremendous losses in the chaotic battle over the past day and night. They even lost two super arsenals! Are they fakes, too?”

“If it is a trap, the enemy is too generous. Even if Lei Chenghu has a way to reassemble the chaotic remnants into a solid battle formation, it is impossible for him to fight against our army with such a weak military force. Besides, no one can take care of such remnants instantly, not even the deities!”

“There’s no need to think about it. Lei Chenghu must be planning to run away. Opportunities are fleeting. Once Lei Chenghu’s remaining soldiers flee to the space jump point and return to the capital city with a space jump, it will be difficult to annihilate them!”

“Deputy General Song, do you still have the courage and loyalty of a soldier of the Star Ocean Imperium? If you don’t, what makes you the commander-in-chief of the Heaven’s Return Fleet?”

Song Bugui’s head was bleeding, but he didn’t dare to say anything.

The data that the intelligence and strategic analysis departments sent back verified the words of the leaders of the military.

The guards of Jade Cauldron sent a lot of clear pictures and videos, including the surveillance of the space ripples, to prove that Bai Xinghe had indeed taken away at least one third of the warships and almost all the trophies of the revolutionary rebels as well as the starships that had just been captured.

Besides, their departure was not a pleasant one. The disobedient leader of the space pirates seemed to have had a minor conflict with Lei Chenghu’s subordinates. It was safe to say that he had evacuated by force and exposed Lei Chenghu’s most vulnerable flank. Lei Chenghu had been deployed in the

flank. The defenseless anti-magnetism warships and the comprehensive supply ships had been handed over to the defenders.

After a day and a night of fierce battles, Lei Chenghu had lost a lot of his crystal warships and almost all his comprehensive supply ships, including two super arsenals. In other words, he had lost all the resources that he had prepared for the prolonged battles. If he did not retreat soon, the starships

would run out of supplies!

The military leaders of the four major families judged other people by themselves. If it were them, there was no way that they would invest so much for a trap.

Therefore, Lei Chenghu was truly defeated. He was utterly defeated!

‘The scanning and analysis of the exhaust flames of the starship supported the idea, too.

The starships were driven by the crystal fuel from the working reactors. When they were cruising normally, the streams of flames spurting out of the power spurts contained a lot of radiation and high-energy particles. They would linger in the vacuum for a long time and leave a trajectory hundreds of

thousands of kilometers long that was invisible to the naked eye.

It was like in the ancient wars where tanks left distinctive traces on the muddy ground.

Although it could not be seen with the naked eye, the senses of the Immortal Cultivators and the sophisticated scanning magical equipment could clearly detect the colorful trajectories.

By analyzing the ‘tracks’, they would be able to locate the direction where the enemy was fleeing to and even learn the situation inside the enemy’s base.

Right now, in the vast space from Jade Cauldron to the No. 4 planet, scattered exhaust flames were everywhere, as if countless headless flies were running and even colliding with each other.

It meant that Lei Chenghu’s control over the fleet had been minimized. He could not form a neat formation and retreat in an orderly manner. All the starships under his command were fleeing toward the space jump point.

More importantly, the defenders had locked onto Lei Chenghu’s flagship.

The power units of every starship were different. Even the same model of warship had subtle differences in the exhaust flames it spurted out after continuous battles, maintenance, and upgrades. One could tell the model or even the number of the starship by the difference in the exhaust flames.

Lei Chenghu’s flagship was a target for everyone. Naturally, it was the primary target for the guards.

When the fleet appeared, the defenders had reported the exhaust flames and trajectory data of Lei Chenghu’s flagship to their superiors.

After a full-scale scan, the fleet soon located Lei Chenghu’s flagship. After analyzing the exhaust flames of Lei Chenghu’s flagship and other starships, they came to a shocking conclusion. Lei Chenghu’s flagship seemed to have been seriously damaged and could not run away at 100% cruise speed. It was

even slower than other starships and gradually fell behind the entire fleet.

“Lei Chenghu is crippled!”

“His starship lagged behind and dragged the entire fleet down!

“His flagship has been seriously damaged. Not only is his speed impressive, his firepower and defense ability are likely to be greatly undermined. If we can catch up to him, it is very likely that we will blow up his flagship or even… capture Lei Chenghu alive!”

The news was like an stimulant that had been injected into the brain of every noble, making their heads creak and almost explode.

Neither Song Bugui nor the ‘Ultimate Joint War Meeting’ had issued an order, but a branch of the Yun family in the fleet had already left the main force and rushed out.

“This, this, nonsense, nonsense!”

In the joint meeting, the leader of the military from the Yun family was blowing his beard and glaring at his subordinates. He seemed to be infuriated by the rashness of his subordinates, but he did not intend to recall them at all.

Therefore, after looking at each other in bewilderment for half a second, the other noble families did not have the time to berate the Yun family for their shamelessness. They hinted to their subordinates in various ways and rushed out in a flurry, determined to stop Lei Chenghu’s flagship before other

families did.

For a moment, the hundred thousand starships accelerated abruptly, leaving colorful exhaust flames behind them, and darted toward the fourth planet like arrows released from a bow. They reached the dark space zone enveloped by radiation, turbulence, debris of starships, and stone belts.

They did not have a unified commander, nor did they have effective teamwork. They even sabotaged each other in secret, sabotaged each other, and blocked the routes of their allies without alarming anyone. The battle formation that was neat when the jump was just completed was now messy and

uneven after several hours of high-speed pursuit.

But in the end, they caught up to Lei Chenghu’s remaining troops.

“Do you see him? He is the ‘God of War’!”

The nobles roared in excitement.

It was like the laughter of a hyena when it saw a dying lion.

Lei Chenghu’s subordinates were indeed in a bad situation.

His fleet had consumed too much armor and fuel in the high-intensity battles that had lasted almost a month. The power units had been wrecked, too, making most of the starships unable to reach 100% of their speed. Some of them were fast, and some were slow. They were gradually dragged into a

zigzagging line.

At this moment, the starship at the front of the line had circled to the back of the No. 4 planet’s orbit. Intense space ripples were spreading out, indicating that it had entered the space jump state.

Many large and clumsy starships, including the flagship, the super arsenals, and the comprehensive supply ships, were still lagging behind like bulls that were breathing heavily and soaked in blood.

Theoretically speaking, if the starship was intact and the space jump unit had sufficient fuel, it could jump at any coordinates. It was just a matter of the odds of success and the consumption of fuel.

However, if the starship was full of barely repaired cracks after a fierce battle, its overall structure would be seriously deformed, and its metal fatigue would soar. Also, if the starship was seriously short of fuel, it would not be able to jump randomly unless it wanted to be torn apart by the

four-dimensional storm, or it would lose all its fuel during the jump and fall into the ocean of the four-dimensional universe forever.

Under such circumstances, they could only look for the area where the three-dimensional space was the most unstable and the natural wormholes were densest, which was the so-called ‘jump point’. That was the only way that they could improve their odds of escaping safely.

It was also the reason why Lei Chenghu had to flee to the fourth planet.

“Fire. Fire. Fire all the magical equipment that can be fired. Cover our ‘God of War’ with the strongest firepower!”

The nobles were beaming with joy. They were too proud to care that they had not reached the effective killing range. Countless dazzling pillars of light pierced through the sea of stars like javelins and spears and turned into thousands of expanding bubbles around Lei Chenghu’s remaining subordinates.

Of course, such tactics were not entirely wrong.

Although it was not enough to damage the enemy’s starship, the surging bombardment and the sweeping spiritual energy could not only slow down Lei Chenghu’s escape but also prevent him from performing a space jump.

In other words, the thousands of light beams had hooked Lei Chenghu’s remaining subordinates like thousands of hooks. There was nowhere to run to.

It seemed that Lei Chenghu had realized that he was at a dead end and could not get rid of the pursuers behind him. Therefore, he simply withdrew the battle formation and changed his direction, trying to fight a battle with his back against the fourth planet.

But now that their morale was so low, it was not an easy task for the ‘God of War’ to gather the remaining soldiers and form a solid battle formation!

“Haha. You can’t even finish the most basic troop deployment and can only set up such an incomplete formation. Lei Chenghu, you’ve got this day, too!”

“Don’t be careless. That’s Lei Chenghu after all. Give him another hour. Chances are that he will be able to gather all the remaining soldiers and gather them into an effective combat force. Before that happens, the entire army must charge and completely disperse his broken formation!”

“Charge! With one sprint, Lei Chenghu and the rebels will be doomed!”

“Capture Lei Chenghu alive and sweep across Empyrean Terminus!”

‘The atmosphere inside the meeting was as splendid as fireworks.

“Father—”

As the communication officer, Song Bugui’s eldest son brought the latest news and rushed to the meeting room with an awful face. But he was stopped by his gloomy father at the door of the meeting room.

“What is it?”

Song Bugui asked casually.

“We have further analyzed the space ripples coming from the back of the No. 4 planet. There seems to be something wrong. The data is very messy. They do not seem to be the ripples of a starship breaking away. They seem to be… interference!”

The eldest son said anxiously, “It seems that somebody has set up a large-scale jamming magical equipment on the back of the No. 4 planet to interfere with our monitoring of the space ripples. But it doesn’t make sense. If Lei Chenghu’s fleet was fleeing in a hurry, there is no reason for them to waste

their time setting up a large-scale jamming array!

“Is there something on the back of the No. 4 planet? The fog of war there is too dense. We know nothing about the existence in the darkness. Should we send more scouts and unmanned crystal cameras to go around it and scan it carefully before we make a decision?”

*oh”

Song Bugui swept a glance at the smoke-filled meeting room. He turned around to look at his eldest son and calmly said, “I understand. You may leave now. I will take care of this matter myself. All you need to do is… firmly control the communication room on the flagship and intercept all the

intelligence. Do you understand?”

Chapter 2913: Fatal Pearl Necklace

The eldest son looked at his father’s gloomy face and then at the elite warriors who had been following him in the darkness of the pathway for decades. He understood everything.

He nodded. Gripping the boltgun on his waist, he left quickly.

Song Bugui lowered his eyes. He didn’t say anything, nor did he try to push open the door of the meeting room to dampen the mood. Instead, he tried to persuade the generals, ministers, and committee leaders to be prudent.

Besides, it was too late for him to waste his breath even if he wanted to. Under the direct command of Song Lixing, the flagship below their feet suddenly accelerated, too, and joined the vanguard, rushing toward Lei Chenghu’s remaining troops at full speed.

For a moment, the fleet of the Heaven’s Origin Sector was divided into five sharp blades. The branches of the fleet all accelerated to their maximum speed, leaving no room for maneuver.

“Two million kilometers, 1.5 million kilometers, one million kilometers. Estimated to be engaged in five minutes!”

All the starships were trying their best to run the power units and squeeze every drop of energy from every crystal. The spiritual shield that covered the shell of the starships and the magical equipment cores inside the turrets were all burnt orange. At first glance, they were glittering like billions of

meteorites that had broken through the atmosphere, or like a burning tide that was about to sweep and crush the fragile towns on the seashore.

Inside the assault cabins of the large warships, the Exos who were ready for the assault were all shouting in excitement. The eyeballs of every noble were almost transparent, as if ‘God of War’ Lei Chenghu’s head was already in their hands!

It was true that, compared to their rapid advancement, Lei Chenghu was in a sorry state.

The broken starship, which had been riddled with holes and running out of ammunition and food, had to brake and turn 180 degrees during the high-speed escape in order to form a solid battle formation. It was more than difficult to reverse the mindset of all the soldiers from ‘fleeing’ to ‘fighting with

their backs against the wall’!

‘The fuel of a lot of starships had been supplied to the power units. For a moment, they could not be transferred to the defense layer. The spiritual shield that they managed to raise was extremely lackluster. It seemed that a salvo would be enough to shatter all of them.

Moreover, at the end of the battle formation, several starships were still fleeing toward the back of the No. 4 planet despite Lei Chenghu’s comman4, trying to enter the space jump state by force!

It meant that Lei Chenghu’s control over the fleet had hit rock bottom. One charge might be enough to break up his fleet and stop it from assembling again.

It was an out-and-out massacre!

“Five hundred thousand kilometers, four hundred thousand kilometers, three hundred thousand kilometers, two hundred thousand kilometers, and finally one hundred thousand kilometers!”

The fleet was moving faster and faster. Murderous roars were echoing in the cockpit of every starship.

When the battle formation of the two parties was only fifty thousand kilometers away from each other, the fleet of the Heaven’s Origin Sector was the first to roar. Billions of light beams that were hundreds of times thicker and brighter than before dyed the entire sea of stars white. All kinds of bullets that

carried intense electromagnetic waves were rushing forward like a meteor shower from the deepest level of hell. A lot of crystal suits and space shuttles followed them and marched forward bravely like wild bees!

Lei Chenghu’s barely-built remnants were not enough to resist such ‘imposing’ attacks at all. The Divine Shield Warship, the electromagnetic interference warship, and the last batch of comprehensive supply warships at the front exploded. The super-large crystal reactor that was hit was like a destructive

bomb. In only several seconds, the entire process of combustion, expansion, explosion, dispersion, and obliteration was completed, and it turned into debris that was revolving at a high speed.

Under the cover of the debris, Lei Chenghu’s remaining troops managed to launch tens of thousands of feeble beams of light to counterattack.

However, such scattered and feeble firepower was simply not enough to break through the defense layer of the Heaven’s Return Fleet. Not only was it unable to block the enemy, the entire Heaven’s Return Fleet was also filled with a hundred times more confidence and courage—in other words, the kind of

courage that those who did not dare to fight against lions and tigers could not help but pour into their bodies when they were faced with drowning dogs.

“Let’s go and show the goddamn traitors what we are made of!”

“Lei Chenghu’s head is mine. Nobody is going to steal it from me!”

“Lam the new God of War. Let me make the final decision and destroy Lei Chenhu’s flagship!”

The two fleets maintained the maximum sprinting speed that a starship could bear. It was only a moment from a hundred thousand kilometers to passing by. But in that moment, about 10% of the starships under Lei Chenghu’s command had been blown up. More than 20% of the starships had lost their

spiritual shields and impetus and could only turn around in circles, becoming live targets. The remaining 70% of the starships had been damaged to varying degrees, too. They had completely lost the concept of a battle formation. Instead of fleeing in a hurry, they were wriggling in desperation in the

black desert-like space.

On the other hand, although the branches of the major families were all minding their own business and making their own decisions, the basic battle formation could still be maintained. The ammunition and fuel were abundant, too. The morale of the fleet had soared, too. The situation was hundreds of

times better than that of Lei Chenhu’s remaining troops.

More importantly, through the white-bladed charge, not only had they shattered Lei Chenghu’s remaining troops, they had also cut off Lei Chenghu’s escape route between the remaining troops and the space jump point of the fourth planet.

That was the most important thing.

“Lei Chenghu’s remaining soldiers are indeed vulnerable. The Eagle of Victory is already hovering above our heads!”

On the flagship of the fleet, the military geniuses of the four major families and the candidates for the next God of War all thought so.

Many of them were even looking at Song Bugui, who was craning his neck and craning his neck, with contempt in their eyes. “Do you see? The enemy is so weak. Just now, he really wanted to run away. If, according to what Marshal Song said, he had to tread carefully and cautiously, then he would have

missed the opportunity and let the tiger return to the mountain!”

Song Bugui didn’t say anything, Even the wrinkles at the corners of his eyes remained unchanged.

He just sighed softly, very softly, and withdrew his head.

The fleet continued moving forward.

Having just completed a high-speed sprint amid billions of rounds of cannon fire, the cluster of starships was still maintaining a relatively strong momentum. It would take them at least a million kilometers before they could turn around and launch the second—and perhaps the last—charge.

At this moment, they were still moving forward in the synchronous orbit because of their inertia and the gravity of the fourth planet.

With the enormous mass of the No. 4 planet, the synchronous orbit would be regarded as a giant ‘gravity slingshot’ that would throw them away in order to reduce the consumption of fuel and increase the speed of the fleet to a whole new level. It was a standard procedure in a space battle.

In the meantime, a lot of agile crystal suits and space shuttles had pinned down the remnants of Lei Chenghu’s army, buying more time for the army to gather and sprint.

It seemed that they had reached the limits of the ‘gravity slingshot’ and were about to be thrown out to vent their fury and killing intent a hundred times more.

At this moment, 99% of the people of the fleet no longer doubted the inevitable victory. It was only a matter of time, an hour, or a minute.

Without them knowing, their entire fleet had arrived at the back of the No. 4 planet.

Then, they saw the saber.

The saber that was as thin as a cicada’s wing, curved like a crescent, and glittering as if it could cut a comet’s tail and a planet in half was standing in their way.

“No.”

The nobles looked at each other in bewilderment. It was not until a long time later that some of them realized that it was not a real crescent saber but a small fleet made of thousands of assault warships and destroyers. The distance between them had been reduced to the point that they were almost

colliding. Thousands of exhaust flames had congregated. At first glance, they looked like the aura of a saber that was slashing the waves.

In such a way, the silent assault fleet shrank their reflection waves to the minimum. Then, with the shadow of the planet, the broken debris and the twisted space ripples miraculously escaped the scanning of the fleet!

Right now, the assault fleet, which had been compressed to the minimum, was expanding and spreading its brightness at a high speed, like a saber that was turning from a dangerous whiteness into an even more dangerous color!

‘The most dangerous saber in the entire universe had been unsheathed.

“This is… Bai Xinghe’s fleet!”

Finally, a few knowledgeable nobles noticed the anomaly from the surging data and screamed like pigs being butchered.

Bai Xinghe’s fleet was not the only thing that shocked them.

However sharp Bai Xinghe’s saber was, it was only the size of several thousand destroyers. It was not enough to reverse the situation of the battle.

However, behind Bai Xinghe’s fleet, in the shadow of the No. 4 planet, in the mysterious mist of war, something that looked like a pearl necklace was glittering and forming an almost perfect circle.

There should have been countless stars around the ‘pearl necklace’, but right now, they were the deepest black, as if a part of the space had been dug out, or rather, hundreds of doors leading to the edge of the sea of stars.

“It’s—it’s a stargate. It’s a super-large space jump beacon. Something is jumping over. Countless super-massive objects are jumping over!”

On every starship of the fleet, the cheers that were incoherently excited were replaced by desperate screams.

The alarm system that was used to monitor the super-massive objects and the space ripples was shrieking so miserably that it was almost piercing through the ears of all the nobles.

In fact, without the reminder of the alarm system, the nobles could have stuck their noses to the window and seen with their naked eyes how magnificent the super-large stargate behind the No.. 4 planet was and how magnificent the space jump it was guiding was!

Chapter 2914: Deciding Our Destiny!

It was not that the generals of the four major families had never seen a large-scale space jump before. In fact, they often travelled through the four-dimensional storms themselves, producing beautiful space ripples. But short-distance jumps and super long-distance jumps, especially the super jumps from the edge of the universe to the center of the universe, were two completely different concepts.

It was like throwing a stone from a high place to the surface of a lake. The further away the stone was, the more likely it would splash.

Right now, there was an extremely small angle between the star of the Jade Cauldron Sector and the No. 4 planet. Most of the brightness had been eclipsed by the brightness of the space gate. At first glance, it seemed that the space gate had opened and swallowed more than half of the star, leaving only a hazy gold ring. With the ‘solar eclipse’ as the background, countless golden ‘raindrops’ exploded in the sea of stars, raising bright gold, silver, and colorful ripples. When every ripple blossomed to the maximum, hundreds of glittering light spots would be born in the darkness that used to be empty. They were like the hungry animals that had opened their eyes after a long journey despite their fatigue. It was the surging flames when the spiritual shield and the power unit of the starship were maximized.

Thousands of light spots and starships arrived at the center of the sea of stars!

What was more terrifying was that they were not the first starships to jump over from the edge of the sea of stars.

Three dense square arrays of light spots had been gathered around the star gate. The distance between each light spot was uniform, like a precise cube. In the silence, there was an intimidating aura.

Every point array represented a branch fleet that had been assembled and ready for battle. The super-large space gate had been functioning at full capacity since at least one day and one night ago, guiding batch after batch of unexpected guests to this place. With the cover of the shadow of the No.4 planet, it had completed a fatal jump and assembly!

More or less, that was when Lei Chenghu fell into chaos and began to collapse. “How-how is it possible that we failed to detect such an enormous space gate so close to the Jade Tripod Planet?”

Countless generals of the noble families were whimpering in pain. They were not trying to find out the reason but looking for scapegoats that they could blame.

The enemy was merely playing a small trick by making a batch of unimportant auxiliary starships and captured starships jump near the No. 4 planet, pretending that they were fleeing in a hurry and disrupting the space ripples nearby so that the defenders would not know what was going on.

It was not a very smart trap. As long as the guards and the commanders of the fleet were patient enough to send scouts to explore the back of the No. 4 planet, they would not be fooled so easily.

Even if they could not stop the enemy from jumping, they could at least set up a proper battle formation with the support of Jade Tripod.

It was a pity that Lei Chenghu’s ‘bait’ was so fat and alluring that all the nobles lost their rationality and vigilance and neglected the most basic tactical reconnaissance.

The ‘Highest Co-Communication Meeting’, which had been lively a moment ago, was now as dead as a cemetery that had been abandoned for a hundred years. All the chiefs, ministers, presidents, and presidents were rendered speechless. They could only stare at the ever-changing light beam angrily, especially the ever-surging data on it.

It was—it was the estimated total mass of the crazily expanding enemy.

Judging from the space ripples that the enemy sent out, the total mass of all the enemy’s starships was close to, if not higher than, the size of the fleet. And that was not counting the fleet of Lei Chenghu and Bai Xinghe.

Although the quality and combat ability of a starship could not be equated, generally speaking, the larger the mass of a starship was, the stronger its impetus would be. The starship would have thicker plate armor, more turrets, and more combat units and crystal suits.

The overall mass of the enemy was much higher than that of the fleet, and they showed no signs of stopping. Countless starships were still jumping over and joining the fourth Flash Point Array that was about to take shape. For the fleet that was being attacked from both sides, it was a catastrophe.

Before the nobles reacted effectively, the extremely dangerous uninvited guests had already moved.

The first to bear the brunt was Bai Xinghe’s crescent saber. The destroyers and the assault ships sprinted at full speed, leaving long, narrow exhaust flames that were hundreds of thousands of kilometers long. They were like the blurred shadows when a saber was waving at the highest speed. Three fully-prepared Flash Cubes smashed at the fleet from different directions like three vigorous war hammers.

First, the elites of Bai Xinghe would tear apart the battle formation of the fleet, and then the three war hammers would launch destructive strikes. It was a simple, even unreasonable, strategy.

The entire fleet was in chaos.

Before the two parties docked, the illusive flames of war of the nobles were extinguished by the surging tides inside the stargate.

“How is it possible that such a large fleet appeared out of nowhere?”

“Isn’t the main force to revolutionize the rebels Lei Chenghu, Bai Xinghe, and the old-fashioned imperial guards? Those guys are definitely not the imperial guards. The imperial guards are still resisting the members of the Covenant Alliance in the capital city! Who are they? Who are they exactly?”

“Our enemy’s mass. Our enemy’s total mass is still increasing. Oh my god. How many starships are going to jump out of the damn star gate? Are—are we fooled?”

At this point, there were still countless nobles who were struggling with meaningless questions, as if they knew the identity of their opponents and could curse them to death with spells.

However, in order to fulfill their ‘wish’, before the high-energy particle stream, super radiation, and crystal armor-piercing bullets reached them, countless communication waves that contained information crashed into the starships of the Heaven’s Return Team at the speed of light.

“They’ve received the communication in plain language. What are the audacious traitors trying to say? Humph. It’s useless to say anything now. They are merely bluffing. They are doomed to fail!” The nobles were talking nonsense that they did not believe, too, while they opened the messages that they received.

Although the frequency of communication between the two parties was not the same, and the serious interference on the battlefield had seriously distorted the information, the scorching information was still unfolding on the light beams of the fleet because the enemy was transmitting the same simple message through dozens of different channels.

The enemy’s information was just a pattern. More precisely, it was a battle emblem.

The lower half of the battle emblem was a golden ball inlaid with nine stars that represented the entire sea of stars.

However, a raging dragon was hovering on the golden ball. Every scale was glittering in the blood-red aura, as if it were soaring into the sky.

This is

The Rising Dragon of the Nine Stars battle emblem.

The Star Glory Federation!

Is this the legendary center of the sea of stars?

Bai Kaixin, chief of staff of the federal army, stood on the bridge of Prairie Fire and gazed at the wavering fleet of the Heaven’s Origin Sector through the 3D light beam. There was even more distress and helplessness on his face, which had been frowning all day.

He had long felt that, under the leadership of Ding Lingdang, the Speaker of Parliament who was ‘unprecedented and unconventional’, the Star Glory Federation was gradually developing in a certain ‘unrestrained and uncivilized’ direction. But he had never expected that the entire parliament, or even the entire nation, would go crazy together with Ding Lingdang and make the decision to go all in and march to the center of the sea of stars with all the mobile troops.

The Kunlun Fleet, as well as the Burning Prairie Fleet, the main force of the Star Glory Federation, as well as a large number of old starships that were not suitable for super long-distance space jumps or had not been repaired, upgraded, or strengthened yet, had all been armed and joined the most unbelievable military adventure in the history of the Star Glory Federation.

As the chief of staff of the federal army, it was Bai Kaixin’s duty to remind Ding Lingdang. “Speaker Ding, what you are doing is too risky. It’s—it’s insane! It’s impossible for us to assemble the highest-level troops and fleets in only three to five days. A lot of starships are still lying in the docks! The soldiers and the officers are seriously short of hands, too. Many young men who just graduated from the military schools have never seen blood before. How can they fight a war?”

Ding Lingdang, on the other hand, replied unconcernedly, “As many as we can. As many as ten people can jump over, we can’t afford to lose any of them! The most important thing right now is speed, speed, speed! It doesn’t matter if the starship hasn’t been fully modified or if the ammunition and fuel are insufficient. Our target is the Jade Tripod Sector, a famous resource and heavy industry base in the center of the sea of stars. When we occupy the Jade Tripod Sector, we will consider it a forward base for maintenance and supplies!”

“This is too reckless!”

Bai Kaixin still insisted on his opinion. “A military expedition is a major taboo. Besides, it is a hasty one. One moment of carelessness, and the federal army will be wiped out!

“Chief of Staff Bai, you haven’t figured it out yet. It is not a question of whether or not the federal army is wiped out, but a question of the survival or destruction of the Star Glory Federation!

Ding Lingdang’s ‘stubbornness’ was ten times more than Bai Kaixin’s ‘stubbornness’. “This is not an ‘expedition’, but a war to defend our home. Are you still hesitating whether or not the troops and the citizens are ready when the enemy is at our doorstep?

“This is a real national war. The outcome of the war in the center of the sea of stars will determine the fate of the Star Glory Federation and the civilization of Cultivators. Are we going to leave our fate to the Immortal Cultivators and the puppets of the deities and devils?

“The difference in overall capability between the federation, the imperium, and the Covenant Alliance is obvious. If the enemy attacks right at our doorstep, it doesn’t matter how well-prepared your federal army is or how well-modified your starship is. The only way to win our precious future is to gamble all our belongings and the glory of the federation with the only opportunity right now!

“There’s no need to say anything. This is not my personal opinion, but a joint decision between the parliament and the citizens of the federation. Therefore, just carry out the command.

“Three days. I can give you three days at most. The Burning Prairie Fleet, the Kunlun Fleet, and the starships that can perform super long-distance space jumps in the defense fleets of the major worlds… If possible, don’t let go of a single sampan. Gather all of them.. We will go to the center of the sea of stars, the Jade Cauldron Sector, and decide the fate of us and the universe!

Chapter 2915: I Will Lead The Charge!

Faced with Ding Lingdang’s fury, Bai Kaixin had nothing to say. He tried to resist for the last time in vain. “But—but if we march out recklessly like this, our rear will be extremely empty. Every Sector will be open to attack. The enemy will only need one light troop to launch a sneak attack and occupy our nest easily!

“Even if we don’t go all out, can we really resist the enemy when they are marching close and all our soldiers are hiding in our hometown?

Sure enough, Ding Lingdang laughed indifferently. “In the game of three sides, for the weakest side, offense is the best defense. The number of our starships is the smallest in the first place. If we distribute our starships evenly to eight or nine Sectors, we will be on guard everywhere. We will simply be live targets in the eyes of the enemy. Only by concentrating all our strength into an invincible iron fist and ruthlessly punching out can we win a chance of survival and seek survival in death!

“I’ve made up my mind. Is there anything else, Chief of Staff Bai?”

Nothing else.

“This is… too reckless!”

Bai Kaixin roared helplessly from the bottom of his heart again, but he still carried out his duty as the chief of staff of the federal army to the letter. Just as Ding Lingdang had asked, almost every sampan of the federal army that could perform a space jump had been captured in only several days. The armed forces and the crew members aside, the scene was quite impressive

It was not just because of Ding Lingdang’s aggressiveness, nor was it because it was the parliament’s decision. Soldiers’ duty was to obey orders. More importantly…

Bai Kaixin narrowed his eyes and gazed at the flank of the fleet. Taking the orbit of the fourth planet as the standard plane, on the left side of the fleet, a glittering saber was rushing close. The scattered brilliance of thousands of destroyers seemed to suggest that the stars in the universe had been slashed apart by the saber.

It was Bai Xinghe’s fleet.

Bai Xinghe was one of the original proponents and the main schemer of the insane military adventure.

“Boss Bai… I’ll believe you for now.” Bai Kaixin clenched his fists. The frown on his face suddenly disappeared, and his lips curled into a smile that could not be described with words. He mumbled in a voice that only he could hear, “After all, no matter how much you conned him, you wouldn’t have sabotaged your own son, would you?”

“Is this the center of the sea of stars?”

On ‘Kunlun’, the flagship of the second fleet of the Federation’s expedition army, which had been excavated from the North Pole and was even more advanced and powerful than Prairie Fire, Peng Hai, the Fire Ant King, Wu Mayan… Almost all the Nascent Soul Stage Cultivators and experts of the federation were gathered. They were deeply awed by the boundless view in the center of the universe and could not help but exclaim.

Even the seasoned experts who had been through life-and-death battles for many years were somewhat nervous. The greatest and most thrilling military adventure in the history of the Star Glory Federation had almost all the starships of the federation on the line. 30% of the representatives, including Ding Lingdang, the Speaker of Parliament, had joined the battle.

There was no victory or defeat. There was only life and death. If the expedition army suffered a crushing defeat, the homeland of the federation in the rear would be like a hedgehog that had been skinned. It would have to shiver in the gloomy cosmic storm and freeze to death. There would be no way to resist at

all.

Although the previous strategical simulations had not been too bad, and the enemy had been attracted by Lei Chenghu’s flagship as bait and stepped into the trap cooperatively, it was still a powerful fleet in the center of the sea of stars after all. It was the last combat ability of the families of the four electors who had ruled the imperium for five hundred years. If the enemy was desperate and decided to counterattack before their death, would the federal soldiers who had gone through the trouble of an expedition be able to survive the test?

No one was confident. “Hey, there’s no need to be so nervous. Isn’t this the moment that we’ve been looking forward to?”

Fiend Blade Peng Hai, Li Yao’s senior brother, rubbed his stubbled head and grinned. “Shocking the sea of stars, trampling over the universe, and letting every star in the center of the sea of stars hear our name… This is the greatest glory for cultivators! All this time, I’ve been imagining this kind of scene, but even in my dreams, I never dared to dream that it could actually be realized by me. I originally thought that I would at least have to wait for my son or grandson’s generation before they could march into the center of the sea of stars in an imposing manner. “Thanks to Li Yao and the support of billions of citizens of the federation, we are now standing here and enjoying the dream. Are we going to disgrace our hometown?

“Hehe. In the eyes of the high and mighty nobles and the Immortal Cultivators in the center of the sea of stars, we are nothing but barbarians in the desolate land at the edge of the sea of stars. In that case, fellow Cultivators, let’s show off our valiance as warriors of the barbarians and march forward unstoppably, just like them!

Peng Hai pointed at the hologram.

The light beam displayed the situation of the Giant Soldiers’ workshop deep inside ‘Kunlun’.

Yan Liren, Han Baling, Master Bitter Cicada, Qi Zhongdao… The out-and-out ‘barbarians’ from the ancient sacred world were not nervous at all. They were doing the final maintenance and debugging on their respective Giant God Soldiers, loading the last flying sword and filling the last drop of fuel before they crawled into the spiritual residences of the Giant God Soldiers.

Hum! Hum! Hum! Hum!

One after another, the ultimate war weapons from hundreds of thousands of years ago that had been sealed for a long time sat up. They were humming in excitement in the gaps of their joints.

They were truly fearless.

After all, they had been ‘traveled’ from the ancient times. There was no difference between the citizens of the federation and those of the imperium in the eyes of the ancient Cultivators.

In Yan Liren’s words, “Do the Immortal Cultivators in the center of the sea of stars have two heads waiting for me to chop off?”

The scene made Peng Hai and many other powerful federal parliamentarians laugh.

“Let’s go and make our preparations. Don’t forget that, thanks to the ancient sacred world and the secret realms of Kunlun, the Star Glory Federation has the highest density of Giant God Soldiers in the entire universe. Let the ignorant Immortal Cultivators welcome the storm of Giant God Soldiers!”

“This is the center of the sea of stars?”

Not just the top experts of the federation such as Peng Hai and Yan Liren, even the ordinary soldiers on every inconspicuous sampan were gazing at the brilliant sea of stars and exclaiming, “As expected of the heart of the universe. The stars in the universe are much more splendid than ours!”

While they were exclaiming, their subordinates were not slow at all. All the officers and soldiers knew that they were in a hurry to join the battle. Many starships and carriers were not fully prepared. One third of them did not even have enough fuel and ammunition. They were merely bluffing.

Only with a hundred times more courage and proficiency could he make up for the lack of equipment and resources.

One batch of warriors after another put on their crystal suits and breathed heavily behind the iron armor.

One vacuum armed shuttle after another entered the launch tubes, ready to be launched like cannonballs.

The damage control team, the medical team, the maintenance team… All the support teams were in position, too.

Of course, the staff and communication officers on the bridge of countless starships were all occupied, too.

One of them was Meng Jiang’s grandson, Meng Xiaolang, who had just graduated from the military school and joined the expedition army as an intern.

As an apprentice staff officer, Meng Xiaolang knew the complexity and difficulty of the military adventure better than anybody else. It was almost a miracle that the federal army was able to jump to the center of the sea of stars across billions of stars in only several days. Even he, the insignificant advisor, had burnt almost all his brain cells for the miracle.

Right now, he could only hope for a bigger miracle. Suddenly, cheers echoed all over the starship. It was Ding Lingdang, the Speaker of Parliament of the federation who had come to the frontline in person, declaring the final battle cry before the assault of the entire expedition army.

Surprisingly, Ding Lingdang in the 3D light beam was not wearing the uniform of the commander-in-chief according to the tradition of the federation. Instead, she was wearing a mustard suit that was as red as fire and as thin as a cicada’s wing, highlighting her tall, muscular, and powerful body.

Meng Xiaolang was immediately attracted by Ding Lingdang’s burning aura. This year’s Speaker of Parliament was everyone’s idol, especially the hot-blooded teenagers. Almost everyone liked her.

“All soldiers of the federal army, this is the center of the universe. It is the place where the first batch of modern Cultivators created the Star Ocean Imperium ten thousand years ago under the leadership of the Supreme Emperor. It is also the place where the Republic of Star Ocean established by the Cultivators a thousand years ago fell into darkness.

In the picture, Ding Lingdang slowly clenched her fists, raised her arms, and declared resolutely, “Today, we have come to this place as the rightful heirs of the Star Ocean Imperium, as the avengers of the Star Ocean Imperium, and as the unyielding defenders of the human civilization. We have come to the center of the sea of stars, to the heart of the human civilization, and we will certainly brand this ancient and sacred land with our everlasting glory!

“Look, the enemy is right in front of us. They’ve been so shocked by our appearance that they’re shivering and unable to maintain the most basic formation.

“Then, pull out your blades, hold your steel guns, and summon your courage… Aren’t the Immortal Cultivators most particular about ‘the weak are prey to the strong, and the winner is king? Let’s let these arrogant, blind fellows open their dog eyes and see clearly who is truly ‘strong! “Prepare the artillery. Attack formation No. 3, speed to maximum. I will lead the charge with the ‘Great Fire Dragon Sparrow… The federal army, march forward and crush all the enemies blocking our way in the future!”

Chapter 2916: The Speaker’S Fury

“Well—”

Meng Xiaolang was dumbfounded. He thought that he had heard wrong.

What—what—what had he just heard? The Speaker of the Supreme Council of the Star Glory Federation, the leader who should be in a high position, was actually leading the charge?

It was one thing to be at the frontline, but it was a whole different thing to be at the forefront of a battle formation. In the history of mankind, which supreme leader had been so fearless?

Meng Xiaolang blinked and looked at his companions, who were all shocked and confused.

But when they looked at Ding Lingdang’s blood-red, burning robe on the light beam, they realized that they had not heard wrong. The Speaker of Parliament, who was known as the ‘Red Fire Dragon King’, was really going to be the sharpest blade of the federation!

Meng Xiaolang’s face was still somewhat frozen, but deep inside his veins, nerves, and internal organs, a cluster of strength was already burning. There was no telling who was the first to start roaring. In short, within half a minute, fanatical roars resounded around Meng Xiaolang and on every warship of the federal army.

“Federal army, crush all the enemies blocking our way in the future. Forward, forward, forward!”

“What”

Even all the parliament members and experts on ‘Kunlun’ were shocked. They looked at each other in dismay. No one knew what was going on. “This is too reckless!”

On Prairie Fire, the flagship of the federal army, Chief of Staff Bai Kaixin roared hysterically for the hundredth time, “Who does she think she is? A muscular, simple-minded general? This is our first attack! Our first! We don’t even know the details of our enemy. We don’t even know whether the so-called ‘friends’ are really reliable or whether she has dug a trap for us. She is the supreme leader of the federation, the symbol of the Cultivation civilization, and she is going to charge out without a care? Is there no procedure for such a major event? Is there no need for a democratic parliamentary vote?”

Bai Kaixin burst into fury for a long time. He went through the rules of the parliament and the military laws in his head, only to find, “When the Speaker of Parliament is determined to charge into the enemy lines, the voting procedure of the parliament will be implemented.

It was understandable. None of the predecessors who drafted the federal laws, the basic laws of Cultivators, and the voting procedures of the parliament had foreseen that the people of the federation would elect such a barbaric and reckless Speaker!

“She is too reckless. I have to stop her. No wonder she did not want to give a speech in the cockpit and insisted on doing it in the hangars. She also said that it would be more fun to be together with the soldiers of the grassroots level. Damn the ambience. She is hiding from me because she is guilty. I should’ve known that she was going to make her own decisions!”

Bai Kaixin was furious. He was about to reach out to the Speaker of Parliament, when Ding Lingdang cut off the communication with him.

Maybe, she wouldn’t dare resume communication with Bai Kaixin until her ‘Big Fire Dragon Sparrow’ reached the front of all the starships.

“We-“

Bai Kaixin knew that it was too late to stop them now. Not knowing whether to laugh or cry, he scratched his head and asked, “How did we get such a Speaker?”

“Mr. Speaker, don’t you think that it is too reckless and that you should think twice?”

Inside the workshop of Burning Prairie, Jin Xinyue opened her arms and blocked Ding Lingdang. One third of her eyes were filled with admiration, one third with confusion, and one third with resolution without any hesitation.

It’s better not to interfere with a professional such as Chief of Staff Bai when it comes to the mobilization of the fleet. Why don’t I shine a little bit in the field that I’m best at?”

They will not be able to overturn the resolution that they will fight to the last drop of blood!”

Jin Xinyue was dumbfounded. Her beliefs were greatly undermined, if not shocked.

Pushing Jin Xinyue away, Ding Lingdang strode toward her ‘Great Fire Dragon Sparrow’.

“Right. There is another insignificant, personal reason.

Climbing onto the breastplate of the big Fire Dragon Sparrow, Ding Lingdang had put down the facial cover that had been carved with nine dragons, revealing only a pair of burning, ferocious eyes.
