Chapter 2917: Speaker Of The Federation Is Here!

In the geosynchronous orbit of the No. 4 planet, a torrent of magma appeared. Thousands of starships were savaging their own power units at the same time. On the basis of overloading, they squeezed out 120% of their speed and charged at the enemy at an overwhelming speed.

In the front of the raging tides, a red comet, mixed with an apocalyptic aura, was attacking, attacking, and attacking. Not only was it far ahead of all the other starships of the federal army, it was also going to be the first to march into the shivering battle formation of the Heaven’s Return Fleet before Bai Xinghe’s ‘Arson Fleet’.

At a closer look, the so-called red comet seemed to be a drop of water condensed by flames. With dozens of propellers in the same shape, it was moving at 1% of the speed of light.

When all the fuel was burnt out and the speed reached the limit, the shell of the

red water droplet suddenly collapsed and turned into countless helical streaks of brightness, splashing everywhere. A burning dragon rushed out of the broken water droplet and soared into the sky, revealing its true self in the sea of stars. It was the ‘Great Fire Dragon Sparrow’, the giant soldier that Li Yao had once slain and was now commanded by Ding Lingdang! After a hundred years of hard work, not only was the giant soldier not worn or corroded, it had also been modified with the most cutting-edge technology of the federation as well as a lot of advanced magical equipment components, which made it even more intimidating. The biggest difference between Ding Lingdang and Li Yao was that Ding Lingdang was more of a ‘body fundamentalist’ who valued combat more than anything else and despised the use of blades or guns. She had poured all her time and effort into the arms of the Fire Dragon Sparrow and modified them to be twice as thick as those of the Giant God Soldier. She was truly like a furious gorilla. Dense attack rune arrays were carved on the iron arms that were as thick as the main guns of a starship. Even if they were not triggered, countless burning dragons seemed to be crawling on them. Taking a deep breath, Ding Lingdang gazed at the scattered, somewhat disordered brilliance not far away. They were the spiritual shields and power units of the fleet that were making hasty reactions. Right now, there was no power among the stars that could stop her from blowing up the heads of the scumbags. Ding Lingdang was immersed in a very subtle feeling, as if she could hear a battle hymn from the depths of her body in the silent vacuum. She heard the sound of blood rushing through her veins like a flood, the sound of bioelectricity rushing through her neural network, the sound of her internal organs craving for food, the sound of her fighting will boiling deep inside her brain, and the sound of her violent nature, which had been hibernating for a long time, gradually waking up!

Maybe because of a freak combination of factors, she had been the Speaker of Parliament of the federation for so many years. Sitting in the parliament and her office all day long, her fighting will had been suppressed to the minimum. She was like a dam full of cracks that was about to collapse.

Maybe it was because she missed Li Yao more than anything else, but the news that Li Yao’s fate was still unknown made every muscle and every bone in her body restless. She needed to vent her anger on a few unlucky guys.

Or maybe, it was the right thing to say. For a Cultivator, or rather, any proud human warrior, there was nothing more important than to be clear about one’s belief. Only by resonating with one’s belief at the deepest level could one trigger the power of the source of life beyond one’s mother’s level, beyond one’s mother’s rules, and beyond one’s mother’s core, Nascent Soul, and even Divinity Transformation!

“AHHHHHHHHH!”

Ding Lingdang’s pair of eyes were completely filled with flames. Her honey-colored skin was also covered in sparkling cracks that looked like fiery dragons. It was as if a brand-new, furiously burning, glittering version of her was about to break out of her shell. An unprecedented strength was like a flood that had broken through a dam. From every cell, every nerve, every vein, every drop of blood, and every pore, it madly surged out. Through the Spiritual Abode, it transmitted to every joint and every piece of plate armor of the Giant God Soldier. It had the most perfect reaction with the battle glyphs left from the primeval era of hundreds of thousands of years ago. Incredible ripples surged out. It was as if the brightness of a red comet had suddenly increased by a hundredfold, becoming the most dazzling center of the entire battlefield!

Ding Lingdang roared, laughed, and burst into flames. Her arms that looked like the main guns of a starship crashed into each other brutally like two shooting stars. Invisible ripples spread out to millions of kilometers nearby at the speed of light. All the starships on both sides were blown away by the ripples.

“Ding Lingdang, Speaker of Parliament of the Star Glory Federation, is here. Who wants to fight me to the death?”

For a moment, the entire universe was eclipsed. All the starships were silent, as if they had been frozen in the vacuum by her roar.

Half a second later, the soldiers and Cultivators of the federation who had recovered from their bewilderment all burst into the most enthusiastic cheers and the most furious roars in response to their leader with their soul and their life.

Regardless of whether Ding Lingdang took the initiative to attack or not, this… was a cultivator!

Encouraged by Ding Lingdang, the thousands of warships of the Star Glory Federation that had been rushing at the highest speed suddenly accelerated by 5% as if they had been injected with the most powerful stimulant. Not only were there thousands of galloping horses, they were also like rabid dogs that had been tied up with crystal bombs and were desperate to perish together with their enemy.

More ‘comets’ appeared behind Ding Lingdang. The high-speed assault capsules that carried the Giant God Soldiers were flashing, too. Thousands of pieces were spluttering. The magnificent Giant God Soldiers were tearing the vacuum apart and shining brilliantly.

“Cultivator Peng Hai is here. Who wants to fight me to the death!?”

“Ancient Saint Yan Li is here. Whoever comes up first will be killed!”

“I, the Fire Ant King of the demon race, beg you, heroes of the sea of stars, for a fight!”

Their battle cries spread out in the sea of stars through dozens of common frequencies.

The most barbaric charge of the barbarian army had begun.

100% true.

I guarantee that I will follow you to the death and cover you with the strongest firepower!”

Chapter 2918: Three Monks Without Water

In terms of overall capability, the fleet of the Heaven’s Origin Sector did not suffer too many losses in the previous battle. Their formation was not messy, their spiritual shields were solid, and they had sufficient fuel and ammunition.

In regard to experts, it was not that the Heaven’s Return Fleet did not have their own Nascent Soul Stage Cultivators, Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivators, and Giant God Soldiers. In fact, their top combat ability was even higher than that of the ‘barbarians’.

But in terms of fighting will, or rather, their mysterious ‘belief’, they were simply ‘zero’, if not negative, when compared to the crazy outburst of the enemy.

As the last hope of the rightful government of the Imperium of True Human Beings, every noble valued their own life more than anything else. They were not willing to repeat the mistakes of Yun Xuefeng, Song Yushi, and the commander of the royal fleet.

They were merely taking advantage of the conflict between the revolutionaries and the members of the Star Glory Federation. They never intended to perish together with the crazy barbarians!

Judging from the aggressiveness of the enemy and the assault of their entire army, even if they won the battle, it would be a brutal victory at the cost of their own lives. How could they defend the glory of the imperial court and their family, fight against the revolutionary rebels, and… take back the entire imperium and live a comfortable life again?

They could not die in such an inexplicable encounter! The moment such a thought occurred to them, they were tied up and torn apart like crazily growing thorns.

At this moment, the consequences of the lack of unity among the four families, who all had their own schemes, were fully revealed.

If they had only one fleet, they might have been able to survive the desperate situation.

But right now, there were four fleets. If one of them was in front of him, he might have a chance to run away and return to the Yellow Dragon Sector.

It was a different version of ‘three monks without water’.

In the previous charge against Lei Chenghu’s remaining troops, the branch fleet of the Yun family had been the first to respond. They were in the lead and were the first to break through the enemy lines. Right now, they were also the first to face Ding Lingdang and the other Cultivators of the federation.

The other three fleets were gliding toward the end of the trajectory of the ‘gravity slingshot’ at the highest speed. But right now, countless streams of flames were spurting out of most of the starships in the opposite direction and braking them. Their speed was getting lower and lower, but they had exposed the Yun family fleet to the joint fleet of the arsonists and the federal expedition

army.

The four families both lacked effective firepower cooperation and did not intend to cover the Yun family’s vulnerable flank at all.

It seemed that they did not care whether or not Bai Xinghe’s saber would decapitate the branch fleet of the Yun family, and whether or not Ding Lingdang, who was rushing forward, would blow up the branch fleet of the Yun family with her burning iron fists!

“What is the meaning of this? What is the meaning of this? Where is your loyalty to the imperial court? Where is your valiance as Immortal Cultivators? Why is your fleet not moving forward? What do you want? What do you want?”

In the meeting of the supreme coalition army, the leader of the Yun family was so angry that he was about to jump to the meeting table and curse his sanctimonious colleagues. “We have so many main force warships and so many Giant God Soldiers that have not been deployed yet. Do you think that we are doomed so quickly? Where are the Giant God Soldiers? Why are your Giant God Soldiers not deployed? Why?

To be fair, the furious leader of the Yun family was wrong about other people.

The decision not to advance forward because of cold feet was not the command of the leaders of the military, but a tacit understanding between the commanders of their respective fleets.

The so-called Great Dao of Immortal Cultivators was ‘a war between everybody and everybody’. Every Immortal Cultivator knew the importance of preserving their strength and watching the fire from the other side of the lake. Those idiots who were not adept at such arts had been eliminated hundreds of years

ago.

At the end of the day, most of the Immortal Cultivators, at least high-level Immortal Cultivators, would only listen to their own commands and consider the problems in their own interests. The leaders of the families did not have much control over the commanders of the fleet below them, or even the captains of every starship.

Not to mention that the experts who were qualified to command the Giant God Soldiers had all trained hard for one to two hundred years in order to reach the Nascent Soul Stage and the Divinity Transformation Stage. They were all the dominators of their respective territories. Even if anything happened to them, they would have all the money in the world and a way out. There was no need for them to fight against the Giant God Soldiers of so many barbarians for a broken fleet of the Flying Star Sector.

After a hundred years of training, countless plots and schemes had brought him to his current level. Would he be able to afford the compensation if there were any bumps? If there were a lot of battle credits and resources that they could get, the Nascent Soul Stage Cultivators and the Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivators would certainly rush to attack them. But right now, they were really risking their lives. It was hard to say whether or not they could defeat Ding Lingdang. Even if they defeated the Speaker of Parliament, they would be drowned by the storm-like bombardment of the barbarians—what was their purpose?

After all, as Nascent Soul Stage Cultivators and Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivators, with the help of the Giant God Soldiers, their odds of survival on the battlefield were the highest. Since the situation was so unclear, it was better… to wait and see.

Just like that, the big shots of the Yun family jumped up and down for a long time, but nobody was willing to talk to him. The other big shots all lowered their heads and fell into awkward silence. They are luring our army to charge forward. Otherwise, how could a dignified Speaker of the Federation charge alone at the front?”

They have always been the strongest of the four families. This little brother also admires them to the extreme. The Yun family taking the first credit is a matter of course. No one dares to compete with your brother!”

As for my family’s Giant God Soldiers, they are undergoing emergency maintenance and are being equipped with ammunition. They will be able to attack immediately and provide an overwhelming cover for our allies!”

All the big shots were flattering Li Yao enthusiastically.

The leader of the Yun family was so angry that he almost collapsed his chair.

Gritting his teeth, he was about to issue a new command to the branch fleet of the Yun family, when the dense light spots that represented the branch fleet of the Yun family in the front of the light beam deviated from their original trajectory and bypassed the coalition fleet of the arsonists and the expedition army!

They were… running away!

To be honest, the branch fleet of the Yun family had reached the end of the ‘gravity slingshot’ at the highest speed and was about to crash into Bai Xingjian and Ding Lingdang.

Under such circumstances, they had to make a 180-degree turn at the risk of the starship being torn apart by the inertia and acceleration. They were determined to run away even though their vulnerable flanks would be exposed to the enemy’s bombardment. It was quite risky. As it turned out, the commander of the branch fleet of the Yun family had calculated the distance between them perfectly. When he completed the turn and accelerated toward the plane of the battlefield in a hurry, he was about seventy thousand kilometers away from the effective attack range of Bai Xingjian and Ding Lingdang. It seemed that, if the enemy did not pursue him, his fleet would have a chance to retreat from the battlefield intact.

Of course, such emergency brakes and super turns were not without cost.

Not only did it consume a lot of fuel, the shell and internal structure of the starship were torn apart by the gravity of the No. 4 planet and its own inertia, too. Its defense ability was greatly undermined.

The previously neat battle formation had been torn apart, too. All the starships no longer cared about where they were and fled desperately in a straight line.

As for the strike to morale… Well, it was impossible to strike something that did not exist in the first place.

As a result, the other three fleets would have to collide with Bai Xingjian and Ding Lingdang in a few minutes at a distance of approximately 1.5 million kilometers.

There was nothing but silence in the meeting of the supreme coalition army. The nobles of the other noble families did not even have the time to berate the big shot of the Yun family for his lack of sense of shame. They sent the most resolute command to their subordinate fleets at the highest speed-Retreat! Retreat at a 180 degree angle!

In front of them was an intimidating army of barbarians.

Behind them were Lei Chenghu’s remaining subordinates, who had just been broken through and were still in a chaotic state.

Even a fool would know which one was easier to deal with. “Hurry up and attack Jade Cauldron. We—we will return to the orbit of Jade Cauldron and meet up with the guards there. Then, we will fight the enemy with the defense system of Jade Cauldron!”

Such high-sounding screams were echoing on the flagship of the fleet..

Chapter 2919: God Of War And Fist King

In a vacuum where there was no friction at all, how difficult would it be for the hundred thousand starships, which had accelerated to the maximum speed with the help of the gravity of the planets, to make a 180-degree turn simultaneously? How much chaos would they cause? The question could be solved by imagining how a hundred thousand wild boars running in the same direction could turn around and run away when they heard the roars of the tyrannosaurs. Not all the captains of the starships in the fleet approved of such a timid and idiotic tactic.

However, the so-called ‘uncontrollable’ was just like that. Everybody was running away, and nobody had the time to care about who was on the other side. Whoever hesitated for even half a second would become the last meat shield to face the wrath of Ding Lingdang, the human-shaped tyrannosaurus.

Therefore, even though the captains were planning to give it a try, when they saw the lackluster and even ill-intentioned allies around them, they all chose to make a turn without caring about the stability of the starship and the crazy consumption of fuel.

This was an avalanche-like chain reaction, a vicious cycle.

Run, run, run. All the captains were of the same mind. They did not need to run faster than Bai Xinghe’s saber. All they needed to do was run faster than their allies!

He would break through Lei Chenghu’s broken formation and return to the orbit of Jade Tripod. After all, there were still a lot of heavy firepower units on the surface of Jade Tripod that could provide strong support. By then, he would have much more options whether to regroup and fight again or to return to the Yellow Dragon World.

It was a good plan. However, the captains, chiefs, ministers, and committee leaders had overlooked one thing.

There were two kinds of Immortal Cultivators in the world. One of them was Lei Chenghu, and the other was Lei Chenghu, who was completely different from them and had been forgotten by them. The Immortal Cultivators were even mocked by them for their stupidity.

Right when the fleet changed their route and made a 180-degree turn, shocking changes happened to Lei Chenghu’s broken array, too.

The previously lackluster spiritual shields were now shining a hundred times brighter than before, like burning supernovas. The power units that had been silent for the past few days, as if they had been blown up, spurted out thousands of powerful spiritual flames, making the starships charge forward unstoppably like ferocious cannonballs. The ‘disorderly remnants’, whose morale was so low that they were barely under control, were brought back to life. They moved forward at a high speed smoothly and neatly, forming the perfect cone-shaped battle formation!

The dumbfounded nobles finally realized two things.

Firstly, Lei Chenghu’s fleet had indeed suffered heavy losses over the past few days. He had even abandoned a lot of slow-moving starships. However, the starships that had been destroyed and discarded were mainly maintenance starships and comprehensive supply ships. Although the super-heavy starships that were loaded with a lot of assets were essential for prolonged combat, they were not as important in a short assault.

Moreover, after abandoning the maintenance ships, the comprehensive supply ships, and the clumsy auxiliary warships, the sharp arrows made of Divine Shield warships and arsenals were lighter, sharper, and deadlier!

Secondly, including the flagship, the strength that Lei Chenghu still controlled was not as heavy as before. He had been concealing half of his firepower and defense ability in the high-intensity battle for an entire day and night on purpose. It was not until this moment, when the fattest prey had fallen into the trap, that he unleashed 100% of his combat ability and revealed the sharpest fangs of the God of War!

“Well…”

All the nobles of the fleet would be drowned in their own cold sweat.

‘Deliberately showing weakness’ was easier said than done in a real battle.

It meant that the commander-in-chief had absolute control over the fleet and that the commander-in-chief’s willpower was as hard as steel and his nerves were as cold as ice. When necessary, the commander-in-chief would have to watch the starships under his command that were not protected by spiritual shields being blown up by the enemy without being able to lend a hand in time.

Only by being cruel to oneself could one be crueler to the enemy.

The Immortal Cultivators of the four families could do the latter half perfectly, but they had forgotten that the first half represented the essence of the path of immortality.

Even so, even if Lei Chenghu was cruel enough to sacrifice his own people and pretend that he couldn’t resist a single attack in order to bait the fleet of the Heaven’s Origin Sector, the fact that his fleet had just collapsed and was in utter chaos was a fact that everybody had seen with their own eyes. The nobles could not understand it at all. Even though he was the ‘God of War’, there was no way that he could have gathered so many people perfectly the moment they turned around!

It was true that ‘God of War’ was not enough.

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

But ‘God of War’ and ‘Punching King’ were more or less the same.

On Lei Chenghu’s flagship, the ‘god of war’ of the imperium was standing below the glittering light beams with his hands behind his back. Beside him was a silver iron giant whose head was connected to the mainframe crystal processor by almost a hundred crystal cables. At first glance, it had already melded with the mainframe crystal processor of the flagship and even all the crystal processors of Lei Chenghu’s fleet into a set of strengthened tactical chains as fast as lightning and a super battle network that covered all the stars in the universe.

This was the ‘Punching King’. Different from Xiao Ming, Wen Wen, and even Fuxi, it was an information life-form that was born for war and destruction and trained, upgraded, and evolved toward the goal of being the strongest in the universe.

Only the experience, willpower, and leadership of the God of War, together with the computational ability of the boxing champion and the enhancement of the battle network, could transform the rotten into the miraculous and complete the incredible assembly and assault.

“The federal expedition army is already on the move. All the starships have entered the sub-light speed state, leaving no way out for themselves. How resolute they are!

The crystal cable on the back of the Fist King was shivering slightly, as if the data that was flooding in had a mass of its own. It turned into clusters of scorching magma that were burning its crystal cameras. The Fist King quickly analyzed the data of the friendly army on the other side. Then, he exclaimed in surprise, “The one at the front is actually Ding Lingdang, their Speaker of Parliament? What does that mean? Is it a unique combat habit of the human civilization at the edge of the sea of stars? That’s… unbelievable!”

Pausing for a moment, it quickly calculated and found a plausible explanation. “However, the most unbelievable thing is that she is the wife of ‘Vulture Li Yao’. Everything makes sense now.

Lei Chenghu looked at the rapidly changing information and the pouring data on the light beam. Ding Lingdang’s declaration echoed in his ears, too.

“Ding Lingdang, Speaker of Parliament of the Star Glory Federation, is here. Who wants to fight me to the death?”

Lei Chenghu’s lips twitched. There was confusion and astonishment on the cold-blooded warrior’s face, too.

“Here comes another monster.” Lei Chenghu smiled bitterly and mumbled to himself, “Li Yao, Long Yangjun, Bai Xingjian, His Majesty, and the mysterious, promising creatures of information such as yourself, and now Ding Lingdang, who likes to lead the charge despite being the leader of a military nation. There are more and more monsters in the center of the sea of stars today. It’s getting livelier and livelier.

“General Hu, what did you say?”

The boxing champion asked.

“It’s nothing. I just feel that I’m too old and my head is too rigid. It’s hard for me to understand monsters like you and this… new era where everything is surging and exciting.

Lei Chenghu sighed. “The old era that belonged to me is withering. I can’t keep up with you monsters anymore. It seems that I should find a good exit and leave the stage to you newcomers.

“However…”

The God of War took a deep breath. Although he said that, stubbornness, competitiveness, and bloodthirst were beaming out of his eyes. He stared at the gradually dispersing fleet on the light beam. His eyes seemed to be tearing apart every mystic ray that constituted the light beam.

“I can walk off the stage that I’ve been fighting for two hundred years on my own. But as long as I’m standing on the stage for one second, I will be the God of War. Nobody will force me to walk down the stage. Nobody will be able to defeat me in the blood-stained sea of stars. Lei Chenghu of the imperium!

Lei Chenghu clenched his communicator so hard that it was squeaking. He roared at the soldiers of the entire army who had been suppressing themselves for a long time, “Do you see it, soldiers of the new empire? The foreign army from the edge of the sea of stars has just marched into an unfamiliar battlefield, and yet they are already so bold and brutal. The enemies in front of them, the self-proclaimed ‘Immortal Cultivators’, are so timid and selfish that they have disgraced the Immortal Cultivators!

“We will never admit that the cowardly, ugly guys are Immortal Cultivators. They are just maggots, cockroaches! It will be the greatest insult to the path of immortality if they continue to live an ignoble life! Let’s end the lives of the maggots with our own hands and tell the visiting army what a real Immortal Cultivator looks like!

Before the roars echoed in every starship under his command, Lei Chenghu had already commanded his flagship to accelerate and crash into the fleeing fleet.

Below the flagship, all the starships had minimized the output of their spiritual shields. The arc defense was concentrated at the head of the starship and abandoned the flanks and the rear. In such a way, all the spiritual energy was concentrated on the billions of turrets, unleashing the strongest firepower. Such an aggressive strategy required a lot of control over the starship to ensure that its flanks were not exposed to the enemy’s bombardment.

Or rather, the attackers were 120% confident in their firepower.. They believed that, with a charge and a salvo, the enemy would be torn apart and utterly defeated!

Chapter 2920: : Defeated

Although the sea of stars was vast, the distance of millions of kilometers was covered in the blink of an eye.

In the blink of an eye, the screams of the nobles had yet to rush out of their throats. Ding Lingdang, Bai Xinghe, and Lei Chenghu’s three fleets converged from different directions like three torrents of steel and crashed into the collapsing battle formations of the nobles.

For a moment, countless bridges made of death rays were established between the starships. The chaotic magnetic field resulting from the collision of the high-speed particle stream and the spiritual shield dispersed like a colorful mist of rainbows. The mist of death was extremely corrosive. In the blink of an eye, thousands of starships were riddled with holes and bumps. Shocking holes were even appearing on them. A lot of crew members, assets, and fuel were spurting out of the holes, as if countless tentacles of flames were struggling and cramping helplessly.

The three fleets on the attacking side had completely different combat styles. The coalition fleet of the arsonists was like a ghost saber that was as thin as a cicada’s wing or as invisible as a shadow. They cut into the most vulnerable joints of the four branches of the Heaven’s Origin Fleet and dissected the Heaven’s Origin Fleet layer by layer like a butcher dissecting a bull, exposing the large maintenance ships and the comprehensive supply ships to the fierce firepower of the follow-up fleets. The faster arsenals and destroyers were also lost. Under the intense interference of the spiritual energy, they could not tell where the star and the Jade Cauldron Planet were and did not know where to run to.

Ding Lingdang and the hundreds of Giant God Soldiers behind her marched into the densest area of the starships of the fleet unreasonably and covered themselves with the enormous size of the starship. They reached the bridge, the arsenal, and the engine compartment like burning iron chopsticks that had been stabbed into tofu. In less than half a minute, they could disable a starship and turn it into a cold, dark coffin. Then, they would break out of the shell and rush to the next starship.

It was a rarely-seen splendid scene in the epic battle in the center of the sea of stars.

The Nascent Soul Stage Cultivators and the Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivators of the fleet were all dumbfounded by Ding Lingdang’s bravery and craziness.

Even though they could gather the largest number of Giant God Soldiers, who would be willing to throw the ultimate weapons that could not be mass-produced into such a crazy battle? Besides, hundreds of Giant God Soldiers were fighting on the street inside their starship. No matter what the outcome was, their fleet would be at a disadvantage!

Therefore, without a leader, none of the Nascent Soul Stage Cultivators, Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivators, and the Giant God Soldiers of the fleet were willing to confront Ding Lingdang and the other Giant God Soldiers of the federation face to face. Instead, they tried their best to run away. Then, they ran into Lei Chenghu’s iron plate made of iron and death rays. Different from Bai Xinghe’s sharpness and Ding Lingdang’s craziness, Lei Chenghu was in the most formal and rigorous formation. He taught the nobles a profound lesson about the battle of the sea of stars, the invincible imperial army, and the real Immortal Cultivators! Their sprinting speed was as fast as Bai Xinghe’s, and their firepower was so intimidating that it almost eclipsed the entire sea of stars. However, every salvo of Lei Chenghu’s fleet was aimed precisely. Their hit rate was shockingly high, and they were determined to carry out their orders. In order to maintain the order of the battle formation, they did not intend to dodge even when they discovered the starships of the nobles on their way. They simply crashed into the starships, even if they were to be burnt into brilliant fireballs.

Earthshaking crystal bullets and high-energy particles fell upon the battle formation of the fleet like a torrential rain, as if countless golden swords were slashing the fluffy snow. The weird distortion resulting from the continuous explosions of starships was like the reflection of colorful diamonds. After only one round of collisions, the fleet was already on the verge of total collapse.

Many starships had lost their courage to fight. Their turrets were all drooping, but they were sending all their fuel to the spiritual shields and the power units, trying to run away. Some of them even crashed into each other in order to find the best route to run away.

More crystal suits and space shuttles were planning to fight the enemy for a while, but they were dumbfounded to find that the mothership had run away without caring about their lives at all.

Losing the mothership equaled to losing the hope of survival. The nobles who wanted to cry but had no tears could only curse and surrender obediently. They turned off most of the units around them and minimized the reaction of their spiritual flames, while they sent out ‘surrender’ signals in the public channel, waiting for the verdict of fate.

Several Giant God Soldiers that belonged to the four major families had been deployed to the battlefield. But when they looked at the unstoppable fleet of Bai Xinghe and the arsonists, the crazy Ding Lingdang, and the expedition army of the federation, and the awe-inspiring fleet of Lei Chenghu behind them, as well as their allies who were fleeing in all directions, they all retreated after pondering for a moment and devoted their precious fuel to their motherships.

Are you kidding me? After only one round of fighting, the outcome of the battle could not have been clearer. The soldiers were already on the verge of collapse. The chaos in front of them could not be cleaned up even if Black Star Emperor Wu Yingqi was resurrected. If they did not run away, would they still be waiting to be buried together with the fleet of the Flying Star Sector, which had congenital deformities and postnatal deficiencies?

The firepower of the attackers even extended to the center of the battle formation of the Heaven’s Return Team, which was theoretically the safest flagship.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

Inside the flagship, explosions that sounded like deep-water bombs were echoing nonstop. Most of the soldiers were running away in fear. Some of them even activated the gel that looked like jelly and hid inside like ostriches, hoping to improve their odds of survival when their starship was blown up. They were already prepared to be captives.

Shrieks that could tear the sky apart echoed inside the meeting.

“What do I do? The enemy’s firepower is too fierce. What is going on?”

“Get out of the way! Why are there so many starships blocking our way? Marshal Song, how are you commanding them? Ask them to move aside so that we can evacuate safely!”

“This is all your Song family’s fault. This is your Song family’s territory. Your intelligence is wrong. You have to be responsible. You have to take full responsibility for the crushing defeat in this battle. When we return to the Yellow Dragon Domain and return to His Majesty, this marquis will definitely give you a piece of my mind. Just you wait. Just wait for this marquis!”

“Deputy General Song, Deputy General Song, what exactly are you doing? Aren’t you the commander-in-chief of the Heaven’s Return Fleet and the supreme commander of the Jade Cauldron Battle? Quickly think of a way to send us back to Jade Cauldron Planet. No, directly return to the Yellow Dragon World. Otherwise, watch out for your head!”

Dozens of generals, ministers, and lords of the Yun family, the Li family, and the Dongfang family turned their attention to the Song family and Song Bugui. They vented their fear of Lei Chenghu and the federal soldiers on Song Bugui and saved some of their face.

Even the future candidate of the Song family, Song Lixing, was like a half-dead piranha that had been stranded on the shore. He collapsed in the captain’s seat, breathing heavily, grinding his teeth, and glaring at Song Bugui with hatred.

“…Yes. I will do my best to escort you back to your hometown safely.”

Faced with the storm of saliva, Song Bugui was as emotionless as before. He bowed deeply and retreated with his ass in the air.

“Father!”

In the shadows of the pathway, the eldest son and dozens of other guys who were wearing the uniforms of the soldiers but had bulging temples like killing machines popped up.

BOOM! Another explosion came from the rear of the flagship, but this time, it did not seem to be caused by cannon fire. Instead, it seemed to be caused by internal damage, resulting in a violent internal explosion in the engine compartment. The flagship immediately slowed down. Song Bugui and his son were well prepared. They stood firmly on the deck like nails.

The nobles and generals in the meeting room were caught unprepared. They all exclaimed in anger.

Holding the cabin wall, Song Bugui was standing next to a round virtual cabin window. On the 3D light beam, the stars and the cannon fire were shining brilliantly, making him look extremely gloomy.

“… can’t accept this!”

With a bitter smile, Song Bugui looked at the blood-stained sea of stars outside the window and mumbled, “Juncai, do you believe it? If the fleet of the Heaven’s Origin Sector is a real fleet united by military orders, with the fearless and invincible spirit when the Black Star Emperor established the Imperium of True Human Beings a thousand years ago, we actually stand a chance in this battle.

“Our starships are not worse than the enemy’s, and the quantity and quality of the Giant God Soldiers are not very different, either. In regard to the critical factors such as the intensity of ammunition, fuel, and spiritual shield, our starships are even better than the enemy’s. The three parts of the enemy, namely Lei Chenghu, Bai Xinghe, and the reinforcements of the federation, also have fatal problems such as the lack of teamwork and the subtle gaps between them. The enemy is merely bluffing and concealing their weakness with the craziness of desperadoes.

“Therefore, if I had 100% control over the fleet of the Heaven’s Origin Sector, without the annoying pigs and dogs, and with the entire army carrying out my will to the end, even a mediocre man such as myself might be able to defeat the legendary ‘God of War’!

“It’s truly… a pity. Leading a vast army in a grand battle formation, clashing with a famous general like Lei Chenghu in the most brilliant sparks in an open and straightforward, thunderous confrontation. Dying under the bombardment of his billions of cannons is the highest honor for an Immortal Cultivator and a professional soldier.

“I’d rather die a glorious death than live a miserable life…

“Father, why do you say that?”

The eldest son stepped forward and unsheathed half of his battle saber. He declared resolutely, “A good bird chooses a tree to nest in. It’s not too late yet. Chances are that you will be promoted even further!”

“It’s too late. You don’t understand. Everything is too late. Since the moment the four electors stole the supreme power of the imperium hundreds of years ago, everything has been too late. You don’t understand. You don’t understand!”

Song Bugui heaved a long sigh and wiped his face. He gradually gritted his teeth and said coldly, one word after another, “Forget it. This ugly farce has been going on for long enough. The imperium, the path of immortality, and the civilization of mankind can no longer bear such torment.. Now, let us personally put an end to this clown’s performance!”

Chapter 2921: Old Jiang

“Understood, Father!” The eldest son’s face turned crimson, and his pores were enlarged to twice their original size. He unsheathed his saber and held it upside down in his hand. Looking around at the warriors of sacrifice behind him, he bellowed, “Everyone, it’s time to take a different path now that the situation has turned so bad. Let’s do it!” “Wait—”

A death warrior, who was lurking in the deepest part of the shadow and wearing a bright green combat suit with fish scales, whose skin was as smooth as jade and whose age could not be distinguished, but whose aura was extremely strong, pushed everyone away and walked out. Staring at Song Bugui, he asked impolitely, “Fellow Cultivator Song, let me ask you one last time on behalf of everyone—have you really won over Elder Song Chenglong and Elder Song Feibao?”

The mysterious and powerful Immortal Cultivator who looked like a green jade was named Yun Zhongyan. Strictly speaking, he was not a death warrior of Song Bugui’s small group, but a visiting professor that he had recruited at a high price. The guy was a loser in the internal strife of the Yun family. It was said that he had been brutal enough to kill almost a hundred of the Yun family’s own experts before his escape. He was a wanted criminal with a high bounty on his head. Having no other options, he fled to the Song family’s territory and was discovered by Song Bugui by accident.

Song Bugui had seen through Yun Zhongyan’s potential. With the thought that he was a rare commodity that could be sold at a high price, Song Bugui had surprisingly not handed Yun Zhongyan over to the Yun family or the Song family’s upper echelons. Instead, he had secretly hidden Yun Zhongyan and helped Yun Zhongyan conceal his identity and disguise himself. Song Bugui had even spared no effort in providing Yun Zhongyan with large amounts of local resources to help him cultivate in order to make up for his small group’s lack of high-end martial strength.

Yun Zhongyan had been working hard, too. He had reached the high level of the Nascent Soul Stage in only several decades and could be called a top expert in the entire universe.

Song Bugui and Yun Zhongyan were basically partners in crime. One of them was providing the money while the other was providing the manpower. They were not just masters and warriors of sacrifice.

Song Bugui had raised a lot of such distinguished and disobedient guests. He had always treated them with courtesy and attracted them with benefits. It was impossible for him to force them to mutiny.

Besides, Yun Zhongyan’s concerns did make sense.

As the flagship of the fleet, it was impossible for the Song family to hand it over to Song Bugui without any concerns. Right now, two elders of the Song family, one in the peak of the Nascent Soul Stage, and the other in the Divinity Transformation Stage, were stationed in the workshop that was right in front of them. More importantly, they both had Giant God Soldiers!

Naturally, they were loyal to the master of the Song family and Song Lixing, not Song Bugui.

However, the two elders were purely from the Combat Department and didn’t know the first thing about military formations and large-scale battles. Therefore, they didn’t come to cause trouble.

Song Bugui had gone through a lot of trouble to recruit a Cultivator in the peak of the Nascent Soul Stage as his guest. It was already the limit of his luck and capability. Naturally, he could not summon a giant soldier out of thin air.

The two Giant God Soldiers were the key to the failure of the battle.

If the two elders supported Song Lixing and the master of the Song family as before, even if Song Bugui was able to control the bridge and the supreme joint battle meeting, he would still be annihilated very quickly. On the other hand, because Song Bugui had told all the death warriors and the guest masters of the Song family, he had persuaded the two elders to join their cause on two Giant God Soldiers, which gave the death warriors and the guest masters the courage to rebel.

“Of course, Fellow Cultivator Yun. You don’t believe me when the time comes, do you?”

Song Bugui raised his eyebrows high, as if he had just heard the most absurd joke in the world. “The lives of thousands of people in my family depend on this insurrection. Of course, I have already contacted Elder Song Chenglong and Elder Song Feibao. Otherwise, do you think I would be committing suicide?”

“Rest assured. Everything is going according to plan. Fellow Cultivator Yun, you will lead the warriors of the family and the guest masters to attack the joint meeting and try to take over the bridge. I will go to the two elders of the Song family and their Giant God Soldiers and try to occupy the warehouses of crystals, fuel, and ammunition as quickly as possible. In half an hour, we will be able to take over the flagship completely.

“After everything is done… Lei Chenghu is a man of his word. The giant soldier that he promised to give you will not go back on his word!” “That’s good!” Upon hearing ‘Giant God Soldier’, Yun Zhongyan’s eyes were glittering. He gritted his teeth and unsheathed his saber, too, before he waved his saber and activated the crystal suit inside his Cosmos Ring. “Brothers, great wealth is right in front of us. Let’s go!”

In the meeting of the supreme coalition army, the ignorant nobles were still pushing and attacking each other or yelling at the silent communication magical equipment. They were too anxious to reach out to the collapsed fleet under their command to notice that a lot of invisible, high-intensity venomous gas was being released through the ventilation tubes in the four corners of the room.

It was not until several nobles felt that their head was dizzy and crimson spots were appearing in front of their eyes that they were greatly shocked. They rose up suddenly, only to stumble and fall to the ground.

“What’s going on?”

“Not good. It’s venomous gas!”

“Someone is releasing poison. Hurry—”

Before they put on their crystal suits, the door had been blown open by a weird force. The broken pieces of the door flew in together with almost a hundred powerful Thunder-Palms. After a series of earsplitting explosions, the entire meeting room was filled with electric arcs that looked like dragons.

Such electric arcs were not enough to tear apart the bodies of the Immortal Cultivators, but they were enough to electrocute them and seriously interfere with the operation of their Cosmos Rings, which would interrupt them from retrieving their crystal suits and other magical equipment.

Hua! Hua! Hua! Hua! Hua!

In the sound of rolling iron armor, Yun Zhongyan, the traitor of the Yun family, led a large batch of soldiers of sacrifice who were armed to the teeth and charged in like infuriated rhinos. “Da da da da da da!” A series of storm-like spiritual energy bullets blew the few guards to the wall, turning them into a bloody mess.

One of the unlucky nobles of the Yun family subconsciously jumped up and tried to run away, only to be cut in half by Yun Zhongyan after a shriek!

“By the command of Vice Marshal Song, those who leave their posts without permission will be executed in case they run away and corrupt the morale of the army!” Yun Zhongyan stomped on the internal organs of the unlucky guy, whose blood was flowing all over the floor, and stabbed his saber into the meeting table casually. Looking at the nobles who were all dumbfounded and scared, he tore open his breastplate and revealed the deadly crystals inside. “We are all tied up with the purest marrow crystal bombs. If you pigs dare to move again, we will all die together!”

On the narrow pathway leading to the workshop, Song Bugui and his son were in a hurry.

“The older, the wiser. Only now do I know that my father has been waiting for this day for so many years!”

Admiration was written all over the eldest son’s face. “I didn’t know that you were so resourceful, father. Not only did you reach an agreement with Lei Chenghu, the Marquis of Liaohai, a long time ago, you even recruited the two elders of the family who are the guardians of the flagship and have the Giant God Soldiers. Everything is under your control. You’ve fooled me!”

Song Bugui hastened his steps and replied without looking back, “You are my own son. Why would I hide anything from you? To be honest, I neither reached an agreement with Lei Chenghu nor did I win over the two elders—Song Chenglong and Song Feibao, the two strongest warriors on the flagship, are still in the dark!”

“What?”

His eldest son was so scared that he almost collapsed. “Dad, what do you mean, Dad?”

“Look at how useless you are.”

Grabbing the collar of his eldest son, Song Bugui’s speed remained unchanged as he continued walking towards the preparation workshop. “If even you are as timid as a mouse, how could those death warriors and guest masters follow us at the risk of their lives if we didn’t come up with such a plan?”

“B-b-but—”

The eldest son was dumbfounded and almost choked. “The two elders did not know that we were going to mutiny at all. What—what should we do next?”

“Why are you panicking,”

Song Bugui sneered, “Aren’t we just going to tell them?”

While they were talking, Song Bugui had already strode into the workshop with his eldest son.

Inside the workshop, two gargantuan Giant God Soldiers were ready to go. They looked like two mountains full of blades.

A large batch of crystal suits that had been working together with the Giant God Soldiers had completed the loading and final examination of the fuel and ammunition, too, like sabers that had been unsheathed.

Two elders of the Song family, who had profound Cultivation and unparalleled combat ability, were pacing back and forth anxiously with their hands behind their back, their faces gloomy. As top warriors, they had sensed that something was wrong even though they had not participated in the strategical decisions at the fleet level.

“Bu Gui, you came at the right time. Why has the starship slowed down all of a sudden? Also, the connection between us and the supreme coalition meeting has been cut off. What’s going on? Did the suicide squad of the enemy sneak in?”

Seeing that there was no one else besides their shivering eldest son beside Song Bugui, the two elders were not bothered and asked casually.

“A mutiny.” Song Bugui glanced at the two elders as well as the hundreds of vigorous disciples and grand-disciples around them, before he spoke in a tone that was neither servile nor overbearing, “I have already taken control of the bridge and the engine compartment. I have sent electricity to Lei Chenghu. The battlefield has risen in revolt.”

“What?”

The two elders of the Song family stared at Song Bugui in bewilderment.

They had seen a battlefield insurrection before, but a lone one like Song Bugui’s was truly… hilarious!

Song Bugui kicked his eldest son to his knees and knelt down himself. He loudly said, “This is to let the two elders know that I just ordered someone to secretly destroy the engine compartment, causing the power of my starship to be damaged by at least 50%. It is absolutely impossible to repair it within 24 hours. The enemy will certainly catch up to us.

“In addition, I have also dispatched a large number of death warriors to occupy the supreme joint war meeting. That’s why we lost so easily!

“Elders, there are only three paths in front of you.

“Or, you can behead me and my son right now and clean up the defeated soldiers in person before we fight again. I only hope that your ability to command a fleet is as good as your invincible strength!

“Or, you can kill me to vent your anger and run away. I hope you can find a faster starship so that you won’t be caught by the enemy’s hundreds of Giant God Soldiers and run back to the Yellow Dragon World.

“You can either join me in the insurrection on the battlefield… In the secret letter that I sent to Lei Chenghu a few days ago, I emphasized that the two of you stand firmly on my side and are the greatest contributors to the insurrection on the battlefield. Lei Chenghu, the Marquis of Liaohai, is a man who loves the imperium and values the big picture. Even if it is to stabilize the morale of the people, he will not treat you unfairly.

“Come on. Your heads are here. Decapitation or not, please make up your mind quickly!” Song Bugui ripped open the collar of his marshal uniform. His strength was so great that he tore a few buttons engraved with the three-star battle emblem of lightning. The buttons rolled all over the ground with a clatter.

Stretching his neck that was bulging with veins, he slapped his neck until it was red and shouted at the two invincible experts.

Chapter 2922: You Are The God Of War?

The entire workshop fell into a mysterious silence. The air was crackling like dry ice. While the two invincible experts were still pondering in shock, the disciples behind them were all seething with fury.

“Asshole!”

“Shameless!”

A traitor of the family! “How dare you! Are you not scared of death?” The young and childish members of the Song family all unsheathed their swords and even aimed their guns at Song Bugui and his eldest son.

Song Bugui’s lips curled into a sneer of disdain. He didn’t even spare a glance at the juniors of his family and kept rubbing the artery on his neck, as if he were picking the most suitable place for the two invincible experts.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

Chapter 2923: Billions Of Turrets, Billions Of Stars!

The distance between the two parties was only a hundred thousand kilometers.

But it was also the most refreshing battle that she had ever fought—this was in the center of the sea of stars, where one rider was equal to a thousand!

Ina trance, Ding Lingdang felt that an ancient and mysterious force was surging out of her broken veins, nerves, and cells and filling the ‘blackhole’ inside her brain and her abdomen quickly, giving her the pleasure of being reborn and soaring into the sky.

Ding Lingdang was dazed for a moment. She stepped on the debris of the enemy warship and burst into laughter, which was meant for herself and the soldiers of the federal army behind her who had experienced a qualitative change during the fierce battle, too!

On Lei Chenghu’s flagship.

Chapter 2924: The Dying Imperial Capital

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

Since three days ago, thunderous noises had been echoing in the sky of Empyreal Terminus day and night. It was not the sound of cannon fire, but the sound of countless starships, cosmic armed shuttles, and crystal suits rushing to the battlefield through the clouds, as well as the sound of their falling

debris.

When the sunlight illuminated the planet, which was on the brink of destruction, the sky, which should’ve been blue, was swept away by crimson tides. In the hazy clouds, one could vaguely see the enormous and fuzzy shadows of countless starships, which were the natural phenomena when the spiritual

shield of a starship was triggered to the maximum and the radiation it released diffracted and spread in the atmosphere. It was like a man-made, cruel, and fatal rainbow.

In only three days, the tentacles of the fleet broke through the defense lines of the Empyrean Terminus Sector and reached the orbit of the planet.

Ifa fierce battle took place in the orbit of the planet, the worst outcome would be that the debris of the starship, which had lost all impetus, would be captured by the gravity of the planet and fall into it like headless flies.

‘Whether they were friend or foe, the debris of starships was mostly made of super alloys and special ceramics that could resist high temperature and high pressure. It was very difficult to burn them in the high-speed friction of the atmosphere. Often, they would turn into burning shooting stars and fall

as fast as lightning, becoming the biggest headache for the cities and military bases of the Immortal Cultivators on the ground.

Falling from the atmosphere and in the middle of all kinds of chaotic collisions at an extremely high speed, even the debris the size of a fist was enough to create a crater thousands of meters in diameter. The energy and blast resulting from the crash would be enough to turn everything within dozens of

kilometers into a living hell.

Every second, thousands of such debris would attack the surface of the planet and become the main targets for the air defense of Empyrean Terminus.

At this moment, one of the arsenals that were more than three kilometers long broke through the orbital defense of the imperial guards of the Star Ocean Imperium miraculously like a wounded bull and broke into the atmosphere. Under the high-speed friction, a bright, curved wall of fire was formed

around the warship.

The arsenal warship had completely lost control and impetus. All the crew members inside had been killed by the blast and the super radiation.

But that did not stop it from transforming its enormous mass and kinetic energy into the most terrifying damage.

Countless starships, space shuttles, and crystal suits of the Star Ocean Imperium rushed close and rained upon it, trying to dismantle it in the atmosphere.

However, its size and mass were too enormous. Even though it was riddled with holes, it showed no signs of disintegrating or being obliterated.

However, the head and the tail of the starship were under different stresses due to the different degree and quality of damage. Under the increasing acceleration, they finally exploded and broke into two halves.

The wreck at the head of the warship was moving faster and faster. Finally, it fell into the offshore of the Black Star Continent, raising a tsunami almost a hundred meters high and swallowing several towns along the coastline. The residents of the towns were not able to escape in time and suffered heavy

casualties.

The wreckage of the starship, on the other hand, fell toward the imperial city precisely.

Inside and outside of the imperial city, the dense fire net made of countless lines of fire was not enough to stop the enormous debris. Seeing that it was about to crash into the downtown area not far away from the imperial city, leaving a crater dozens of kilometers in diameter and a blast almost a

thousand kilometers in diameter and destroying a lot of important military territories, including the Senate, a streak of gold suddenly darted out of the imperial city and pierced into the debris of the starship in midair. After only one slash, the debris of the starship was slashed into two halves, followed

by almost a hundred glamorous slashes that were as fast as lightning and indistinguishable to the naked eye. The debris of the starship was all broken into pieces more than ten meters in diameter and finally destroyed by the air defense fire net, turning into brilliant fireworks.

In the middle of the fireworks, the gold brightness gradually solidified. In the eyes of billions of residents of the capital city, it turned into an awe-inspiring giant soldier and a golden lion.

“It’s… His Majesty!”

“His Majesty has taken action personally for our safety!”

“His Majesty slashed the debris of the enormous starship into pieces with only one slash. Strong. Absolutely strong!”

Chapter 2926: Battlefield Between Reality And Reality

Ultimate Rescue.

It was sleeping, nurturing, and thinking.

It felt that it had been divided into three distinct layers.

The outermost layer was endless data, including the furious data of the battle in the sea of stars in the capital city, the virtual emotions and willpower data of countless laboratories of human nature, and the random data that could predict and control the next ten thousand years based on the preliminary

data.

The middle layer was filled with glittering, unpredictable memories. It felt that it had been mixed with the memories of thousands of people. ever-changing, and endlessly mysterious forms.

and information in the universe. It was a group, a civilization, a universe, and… a god.

Data, information, integration, clusters, civilizations, universes, gods… Behind the man-made concepts, who was it exactly and what was the significance of it all?

“Who am I? Where am I from? What is my mission? Where am I going?

It pondered, pondering with every second and every ten thousand years.

The question as to where it came from did not seem to be complicated. It was created by some intelligent carbon-based creatures and had two main creators.

‘The first type of carbon-based intelligent life had highly-developed biological brains. Their brains had evolved to the point where they could freely release brainwaves for super long-distance and super large-scale telepathic thoughts.

However, such an ability was both a gift and a curse. The stronger the brain was, the more energy it would consume, and the larger space and volume it needed to cool down. Therefore, the carbon-based intelligent creatures had to eat all day long and even evolved clumsy bodies that were almost twenty

meters tall to digest the food and run the cold blood. In other words, they had become slaves of their own brain. Their enormous and precise bodies reduced the screening range of their habitable planets by dozens of times, and their lives became extremely fragile and greedy. Even absorbing crystals

directly was not enough for them. In the end, their brains would absorb too much nutrition, and their other organs would dry up and die.

‘They were Pangu.

The other kind of carbon-based intelligent creatures were smaller in size and better at changing their body size and even their physiological structure according to the environment. They were natural-born experts in gene modification. They even had an organ named ‘Gene Bar’ growing on their tail that

could perfectly combine flesh and blood with metal machines.

But at the same time, their gene strands were very unstable. The mutations and outbreaks of various viruses on their bodies were dozens of times faster than those of other carbon-based life forms. Large-scale plagues often took place inside their species, turning them into unrecognizable mutants and

monsters.

Compared to Pangu, they were more irascible, full of desires and ambitions, like bouncing sparks.

Their name was Nuwa.

depend on you, Nephew Lu!

He thought carefully for 0.1 seconds.

“AHHHHHHHHHHHHHHH!”

They turned into two streams of super-hot matter that were colliding, They turned into two planets that were in the same orbit and moving toward each other. They turned into two clusters of nebulas that were swallowing each other and two black holes that were dragging and tearing each other.

Stepping on the nebulas, they held the stars in their hands and tossed them at each other like burning swords and spears. Their battlefield covered the entire universe, and their bodies surpassed the limits of time and space. They were engaged in an eternal competition.

In the meantime, when all the stars in the universe were eclipsed by their fury, their combat would be reduced to a very specific level.

They had been tangled in the sulfuric ocean that was full of pillars of smoke and fungi and evolved into ancient sharks

The defenders could exploit the resources of the entire planet and the resources of the attackers. Although they would risk damaging the magnetic field and the atmosphere of the planet, it would take decades or even hundreds of years before the risk was fully realized.

Chapter 2930: Forbidden Scale
