“Qingchen, run while I have the last bit of strength to control it!”

The giant golden net turned out to be Li Yao, a small earthworm. The high-intensity soul burning put Li Yao on the verge of death, too. He revealed his head in front of the giant golden net and shouted at Lu Qingchen desperately, “Run! We’ll fight for the ultimate path later. Right now, before I die, run!

Run! Run now!”

“You want to run away? Not so fast! Last time, when I swallowed the soul fragment of you as a ‘Nether World Devil’, my Cultivation soared and evolved to an unbelievable new level. This time, after I absorb all your remaining life information, what kind of amazing changes will happen? I’m really looking

forward to it. Hahahaha. Hehehehehe!”

The mental devil cackled. The body embedded in the golden net suddenly expanded. The black venom and the bloody mist that it spurted out corroded the golden net like acid.

“Pa! Pa! Pa! Pa! Pa!” Amidst Li Yao’s blood-curdling screams, the golden net broke apart inch by inch. It was as if a small earthworm that had been stretched to the limit had been slashed into over a hundred pieces. “Tragic” was not enough to describe it!

“Qingchen, I can only… help you… to… this…”

Countless scraps of Li Yao’s soul smiled feebly.

In the next second, they were swallowed by the soaring flames of the mental devil.

The mental devil was not satisfied after swallowing Li Yao. Instead, its hunger was aroused, and it charged at Lu Qingchen a hundred times faster than before like burning black magma.

Lu Qingchen had witnessed the formidability and horror of the mental devil with his own eyes. Logically speaking, as long as he still had the last bit of rationality, he should flee and make plans.

But he seemed to have been scared out of his wits. His face was gloomy, his eyes were blinking, and his lips and the corners of his eyes were twitching. The billions of thoughts that constituted the giant of light were flickering and changing into thousands of colors and patterns.

‘When the mental devil accelerated to the maximum speed and could not turn back anymore, Lu Qingchen finally made up his mind.

Not only did he not run away, he even condensed all the streaks of brightness around him into an unstoppable blade!

“ant”

Inside the light blade, Lu Qingchen’s eyes were bloodshot, revealing the craziness of the last gamble!

“Huh?”

In the middle of the black streaks of brightness made of the bloody heart, a shocked scream echoed, which was completely different from the rampant, brutal, and dominating voice just now.

BOOM!

Two clusters of information streams that seemed to be equally powerful crashed into each other brutally, resulting in a blast that enveloped the entire virtual space. Then, they were separated by the surging blast.

‘When the swirl calmed down and everything was settled, the mental devil was still occupying the center of the ocean of data like a glutton, and Lu Qingchen turned from a blade of light into a giant of light again.

At first glance, the two ‘Unparalleled Devil Lords’ did not seem to have changed at all. They were merely exchanging positions.

Moreover, Lu Qingchen’s soul had been seriously damaged. Not only was his body size much smaller, even his flames were much dimmer, too. He seemed to have suffered a great loss.

But Lu Qingchen’s lips curled into a vague smile, while the Taotie-like mental devil was… shivering.

“Chi

The mental devil tried its best to maintain its arrogant and brutal appearance, but it could not hold on for more than three seconds. The sucking discs and wrinkles all over its body were spurting out useless garbage data, which was shrivelling and shrinking like a deflating ball. It was even rolling in the

ocean of data because of the counterforce.

God knows how much garbage data this guy had stuffed into the framework of his own soul. He was simply a straw bag with a stomach full of straw. When all the garbage data had been spat out, this guy had shrunk to the size of Li Yao a moment ago. Where was the imposing aura of a Taotie, a prehistoric

behemoth, a nightmare beast, and a Dark Lord?

No. It was not ‘almost. It was more like a mirror. Right now, it was bouncing in front of Lu Qingchen’s eyes and twisting into two earthworms, one gold and the other red. The two earthworms were both waving their sparse flagella and looking at him innocently. Although their faces were gone, they were

still smiling in embarrassment and flattery.

Everything was clear now.

It was true that Lu Qingchen had not fully recovered from the damage caused by the self-detonation of his soul. Having just been reborn, he was like a newborn baby who had swallowed ‘food’ that far exceeded the limits of his digestion. Naturally, he could not bring out his full combat ability and had to

resort to the dazzling effects of sound and electricity to bluff.

However, no matter how much he bluffed, there was still a limit. At most, he could only boast of 30% of his strength. In other words, even if he completely leaned towards the dark side, Lu Qingchen still had a bottom line. He needed some face.

The other two—Li Yao and the mental devil—were utterly shameless..

Chapter 2949: Last Punch

They were two sides of the same coin. In the battle against the Fuxis just now, in order to perform the ultimate skill ‘Earth Shock Wave’, their souls had been drained together.

However, in order to fool Lu Qingchen, the mental devil still swallowed a lot of meaningless data while Li Yao was dealing with Lu Qingchen. It then packed the data layer by layer, compressed it, and pretended that it had a strong soul. It even played a trick on Li Yao, trying to scare Lu Qingchen away.

To be honest, for a moment, Lu Qingchen’s defense was indeed on the verge of collapse. He was almost fooled by their bluff.

If it were an opponent that Lu Qingchen did not know or had a good reputation, he might have been fooled and run away.

However, since his opponents were Li Yao and the mental devil, and considering their sordid past and their sordid tricks, Lu Qingchen made up his mind to risk it all.

As he expected, he had seen through Li Yao and the mental devil.

Li Yao and the mental devil could not be blamed for their poor acting skills. They could only blame themselves for using the same trick too many times and leaving a solid impression.

In the last battle of the federation, they had performed similar tricks in front of Lu Qingchen and Mo Xuan, which gave Lu Qingchen a deep understanding of their character.

Lu Qingchen’s smile was more and more scornful and scornful, with a hideous look on his face.

Li Yao and the mental devil looked at each other and swallowed at the same time.

“Like I said, it won’t work. It is just a waste of time!”

‘The mental devil was crying miserably.

“Who says it won’t work? It’s obvious that your acting skills are too exaggerated, alright? Why do you always have to play the role of an arrogant, despotic, and incomparably evil devil? Why do you have to make a bloody mouth that is full of fangs? Of course, people won’t believe such an exaggerated and

imaginative appearance!”

Li Yao could not help but complain.

“am I exaggerating? Are you kidding me? My acting skills are absolutely superb. You must’ve given yourself away. When you screamed in my mouth, it was as fake as it could get. Do you know how much I wanted to laugh? You should reflect on your own acting skills before you blame me!”

The mental devil mocked back.

“What’s wrong with my acting skills? Let me tell you, I, Vulture Li Yao, have roamed the sea of stars for a hundred years. I’ve fought all over the universe, and I’ve never met a match. I rely solely on my acting skills to make a living! You can insult my strength, you can even insult my personality, but you

absolutely can’t insult my acting skills! Also, when it comes to laughter, what’s the meaning of your ‘hehehehehehe’ laughter? Yes, there’s an idiom called ‘hehehehehehehe’, but ‘hehehehehehe’ doesn’t equate to letting out a strange laughter like ‘hehehehehehe’. Do you see any normal person laughing like

‘hehehehehe’? Just hearing this kind of laughter is very out of character. You can see through the essence of your hypocrisy in a flash!”

The more Li Yao spoke, the angrier he became.

“So, you are trying to push all the blame to me now? You were the one who insisted that I demonstrate the aura of the “Tyrant of the Dark Universe’ and that I should be a hundred times more evil than him so that he would realize the consequences of his impulsiveness and feel deeply regretful! If I didn’t

laugh in such a way, would I have laughed in such a way? What kind of ‘Tyrant of the Dark Universe’ is that?”

The mental devil was roaring more and more furiously, too.

“I did ask you to be a bit evil, but I didn’t ask you to be so exaggerated. Don’t tell me that you can’t even accurately grasp the subtle difference between the two. If you need me to teach you how to grin hideously, then what’s the point of having you? Also, in the end, why did you rush over so quickly? You

didn’t even give him a few seconds to think before you rushed over in a hurry to swallow him. Then, he would have acted out of reflex and instinct. Of course, he would have taken action to defend himself! If you were a bit slower and gave him a few seconds to think, maybe he would have reacted and run

away with his tail between his legs? What a useless thing!”

Li Yao gnashed his teeth in hatred.

“Are you kidding me? I risked my life for you and risked my life for him. He stabbed me dozens of times just now. How can you say such things to me right now? Alright. Since you are so dissatisfied with me, let’s break up. We might even have a life-and-death battle. Even if I am going to be completely

destroyed by him in one minute, I will kill you first, you ungrateful bastard!”

The mental devil shrieked.

“Come on then. I’ve been meaning to eliminate you for a long time, too. Come on. I’ll count to three. Let’s do it together. Whoever doesn’t do it is a coward!”

Li Yao roared.

“Alright, then let’s fight to the death. Nephew Lu, don’t stop me. The matter between us can be settled later. Everything can wait until 1 kill this scumbag!”

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

The mental devil was more than infuriated. No. It was more like a furious whip.

For a moment, the two earthworms had expanded into giant boas. Their flagella were shivering at a high speed, as if they were covered in propellers.

Li Yao and the mental devil glared at each other. Taking a deep breath, Li Yao bellowed almost simultaneously, “Three!”

The moment the word ‘three’ left his mouth, the two earthworms turned into two streaks of gold and red and dashed out.

However, it was not aimed at each other but in two completely different directions.

Li Yao grabbed Xiao Ming, while the mental devil picked up Wen Wen. The rapidly-revolving ‘whip propeller’ boosted their speed to the maximum, pushing them to the edge of the ocean of data in 0.1 seconds.

But right then.

‘The two dazzling auras seemed to have foreseen that they would run away. They had been waiting at the edge of the ocean of data for a long time.

At this moment, he slashed at the two earthworms brutally, forcing them to retreat in embarrassment.

One slash after another, the aura of the saber was like a series of thunderbolts, forcing the two earthworms back to Lu Qingchen.

Li Yao, the mental devil, and Lu Qingchen looked at each other in bewilderment. There was nothing but dead silence and awkwardness.

The expression on Lu Qingchen’s face was not so much one of self-satisfaction as one of embarrassment for Li Yao and even for himself, who had worshipped Li Yao a hundred years ago.

Li Yao, whose flagella had all been cut off, didn’t know what to say, either. He could only cough dryly and shamelessly said, “Qingchen, listen to me slowly explain. Actually, the whole thing is not what you think it is. I didn’t mean to trick you. I just wanted to use this harmless interlude to tell you some of

the principles of being a human being—we really can’t control everything. Look, didn’t I not control it well and mess things up?

“The acting is false, but the logic is true. Don’t be entangled by your own dark side. Otherwise, you will end up like me right now. You will be burdened by this mental devil and can’t get rid of it no matter what. How miserable!”

“Don’t listen to him, Mr. Lu. He is trying to trick you so that he can run away and find his wife to beat you up!”

The mental devil shouted, “Even I had to deceive you against my conscience because of his tyranny. But now, I’ve thought it through. Really. I’ve thought it through. Joining hands with you is not a bad idea. With my wisdom and your combat ability, who else in the entire Pangu Universe is a match for us?

Hehehehe. Don’t you agree?”

“Qingchen….”

Li Yao said.

“Nephew Lu…”

The mental devil said.

Several veins on the temples of the giant of light that Lu Qingchen had turned into seemed to be broken instantly.

“You are a disgrace to the federation. Let me send you to the deepest level of hell so that you won’t keep embarrassing yourself!”

Lu Qingchen screamed hysterically with all his strength. All the laws of the data ocean turned into lightning that wreathed his fists. His heavy fists were like tornadoes that were carrying countless sharp blades. They were aimed at Li Yao and the mental devil, only to engulf the two of them.

Even when he was faced with the Fuxis, Lu Qingchen had never been so furious. He was so furious that he did not care whether or not he could digest the Fuxis perfectly. He was determined to beat Li Yao into a pulp.

Tm screwed!

Li Yao and the mental devil exclaimed at the same time. This time, they did not have any trump cards. They could only watch the apocalyptic tornado grow larger and closer.

Of course, even just now, the two of them were not trump cards at all. They were merely stalling for time, hoping to buy more time

Right when the tornado that was full of sabers and swords was about to tear Li Yao and the mental devil apart, an earsplitting explosion suddenly echoed above their heads, as if a burning meteor had fallen into the ocean and almost emptied half of the data space. The immense blast seemed to be piercing

through the entire virtual world!

As the blast spread out, the entire ocean of data was burning, The seawater turned into magma, which was boiling and exploding nonstop.

An unexpected stream of super-compressed data hit Lu Qingchen’s tornado precisely like a black meteorite and blew it into pieces.

Then, the meteoroid stopped right in front of Li Yao and the mental devil and helped them resist the attack of the giant of light.

Accompanied by an earsplitting noise that sounded like a functioning machine, the black meteorite gradually unfolded and turned into a magnificent iron giant covered in dense, complicated runes. The features of the runes were different from those of all the runes in the history of the Pangu Universe.

They seemed to be from… a different universe!

“This is—”

Looking at his broken and slowly recovering right arm, Lu Qingchen stared at his new opponent in disbelief.

Scanning the database of himself and the Fuxi Clan, he could not find the files of such a strong enemy.

“Punching King, save me!”

Li Yao and the mental devil were crying tears of joy. The person they had been waiting for was finally here.. Except for Fuxi, Xiao Ming, and Wen Wen, the last powerful information life-form in the Pangu Universe!

Chapter 2950: Cross-Border Strike

Sensing the surging strength of the enemy, especially the fact that he could summon the wind and summon the rain in the virtual space and even make use of the natural laws of logic, Lu Qingchen’s face was extremely awful.

“Punching champion? What boxing champion?”

He mumbled to himself and searched the database multiple times, only to find nothing about the Fist King.

It must be noted that this was a virtual space created by the super artificial intelligence named ‘Fuxi’ hundreds of thousands of years ago as the core of the mind. Right now, most of the external data interfaces were under his control. It was impossible for ordinary human beings, even if they were in the

Divinity Transformation Stage, to break in easily.

Only two kinds of people, or rather, two weird life forms, could enter this place.

The first was the artificial intelligence such as Fuxi, Xiao Ming, and Wen Wen.

The second type was Lu Qingchen himself, Li Yao, and Professor Mo Xuan, who were weird existences between human beings and information lifeforms.

It was also why Lu Qingchen dared to dawdle with Li Yao for so long.

He did not expect that he would also smell a familiar scent from the ‘Punching King’, as if they were… the same kind!

‘While Lu Qingchen was observing the Fist King, the Fist King was also observing Lu Qingchen carefully from top to bottom.

“Very good. Very strong.”

The boxing champion said straightforwardly, “I like it.”

The boxing champion was an artificial intelligence born for the purpose of “becoming the strongest in the universe’, just as “protecting the civilization” was the core driving force for Fuxi and Lu Qingchen. “Defeating all enemies and becoming the strongest in the universe” was also the core driving force,

or rather, the “belief in one’s heart’.

Ina sense, the boxing champion was a warrior who was purer than Li Yao, Lu Qingchen, or even Ding Lingdang. How could he not be excited when he saw the raging Lu Qingchen?

“Damn. He is bluffing again. Is he trying to play the same trick again?”

Lu Qingchen gritted his teeth. He felt that it was another of Li Yao’s tricks. He bent down like a dragon that had been compressed to the minimum. The brightness around him tumed from white to orange. “No matter how many times I try, I will not be fooled!”

“Qingchen, believe me, I’m not bluffing this time. His name is Fist King. He is also a strong artificial intelligence who specializes in combat. He is really good at fighting!”

Li Yao craned his head out of the Fist King’s back like an earthworm and said earnestly, “You’ve just been reborn and haven’t digested alll the data and information about the Fuxis yet. The ‘Fuxis’ data’ are stuck in the depths of your soul like rocks, which is a great burden for you. Under such circumstances,

you are definitely no match for the Fist King, Really. Uncle Li is doing this for your own good. You don’t stand a chance!

“It’s true. It’s true. I can prove that, although the guy has always been shameless and full of bullsh*t, he was not lying to you this time.

The mental devil crawled out of the other shoulder of the Fist King, too, while it wriggled and said, “Nephew Lu, stop trying to kill yourself. Just surrender!”

“Shut up, you two noisy bastards! Shut up now!”

Lu Qingchen’s eyes were bloodshot, and he was on the verge of collapse. He shouted desperately, “It’s hilarious. Professor Mo Xuan was lamenting that the real strong artificial intelligence, the information life, could not be born in a few hundred years. He did not expect that one or two information life

would pop up in such a short time. First, it was Fuxi, then it was Xiao Ming and Wen Wen. Now, there is a boxing champion? How is it possible?”

“Well…”

Li Yao thought for a moment and said, “Qingchen, have you ever heard of the ‘cockroach theory? If you find a cockroach in your house, you will be sure that it is not the only one. Chances are that cockroaches have been crawling all over the corners of the house. You just didn’t notice them.

“The so-called ‘information life’ has the same logic as cockroaches. Life is never born alone. Maybe the appearance of every life represents the bud of a vast civilization. Now that there is a Fuxi, there will certainly be a second, a third, or even thousands of others!

“Anyways, don’t force yourself. Just surrender. There’s no shame in surrendering to your Uncle Li. Back in the day, so many heroes wanted to surrender to me. Black Star Emperor Wu Yinggi and so on. I didn’t even look him in the eye before I kicked him away. That’s you, Qingchen. Seize the opportunity

and cherish your life!”

“What Roach Plan? I think you are the biggest cockroach in the sea of stars. I will never surrender to a sleazy cockroach like you!”

Lu Qingchen roared hysterically. Two tornadoes burst out of his arms again. He squeezed out the last bit of strength in his soul and the last bit of data in his database and threw them at the Fist King. “Nobody can stop me, not you, the cockroach, or the ‘Punching Kin

Hu!

The two storms of destruction were like countless broken virtual worlds, countless miserable days of destruction, and countless hells made of mountains of corpses and seas of blood. They turned into a hundred and eight thousand mountains and pressed down on the Fist King, Li Yao, and the mental

devil.

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

Li Yao and the mental devil fled like rats.

The boxing champion’s eyes, on the other hand, were filled with soaring flames of war. Every rune on his body was burning furiously. His right arm was deformed and expanded, as if all the flesh, blood, or data of his body had been gathered in his right arm. He aimed at the point where Lu Qingchen’s

strength was the strongest and punched it brutally.

BOOM!

Lu Qingchen collided heavily with the core database of the boxing champion.

Neither side left any room for negotiation. Their first move was their strongest killing move.

It was not just the collision of two high-speed crystal trains or two starships. It could not even be described as a meteor crash. It was more like a four-dimensional jump of a star to the body of another star. It was indeed an apocalyptic collision.

For a moment, Lu Qingchen and the Fist King seemed to be fighting in different identities and forms in the billions of broken virtual worlds, just like Li Yao and Fuxi did before.

They blew out overlapping blast waves in every virtual world. The blast spread out of the virtual world and turned into turbulence and swirls in the ocean of data, blowing Li Yao, the mental devil, Xiao Ming, and Wen Wen to the ground.

Li Yao, the mental devil, Little Ming, and Wenwen, the two earthworms, and the two small plankton, had to huddle together in order to resist the super storm that the Fist King and Lu Qingchen blew out. They felt that a long time had passed, but it also seemed that only one second had passed before the

storm gradually died down.

“Who… actually won?”

Li Yao’s heart was beating fast. He extended a newly-borm flagella and swam toward the depths of the ocean of data cautiously.

He saw that the Fist King’s enormous iron body was curled into a ball, as if his limbs and bones had been burnt into iron slag under a high temperature of more than ten thousand degrees. The glamorous and shining runes were all lackluster and peeling off. There were holes and cracks all over his body,

making him look extremely miserable.

In the meantime, Lu Qingchen’s giant of light was still showing off in front of him, Although the brightness around him was much dimmer, he was still much better than the boxing champion, not to mention Li Yao and the mental devil.

“Are you serious?”

Li Yao secretly clicked his tongue. “Lu Qingchen has become so strong that even the boxing champion is no match for him?”

“Hehe. Hehehehe.”

Lu Qingchen chuckled in a low voice. The giant of light was glittering evilly. He looked at the Fist King and said, “You are indeed strong. Those two pretentious cockroaches are no match for you. It’s a pity that…”

His voice came to an abrupt end. His face suddenly turned extremely weird and confused. He stared at the boxing champion, unable to say anything.

The boxing champion looked at him in silence, too.

Li Yao and the mental devil both sensed a weird scent from the depths of Lu Qingchen’s soul, as if a force that did not belong to the Pangu Universe was surging out.

“This is—thii

Lu Qingchen looked as if ten thousand fleas were crawling in the depths of his soul. He controlled the giant of light to scratch and even tear his own body, tearing ugly cracks on his translucent body, but to no avail.

Very soon, a fuzzy object popped up from the depths of his soul and gradually spread out on the surface of his shining body like a dazzling stripe.

It was a rune, or rather, a rune array.

It was not Lu Qingchen’s own, or rather, the unique Cultivation rune arrays of the Pangu Universe.

Instead, they were rune arrays carved with hexagrams and words that looked like earthworms and tadpoles.

BOOM!

The first rune array exploded on the surface of Lu Qingchen’s soul. A crazily burning meteor seemed to have pierced through space and hit Lu Qingchen directly!

The giant of light staggered. After the explosion, a large part of his body tissue, or rather, his database, was gone. A lot of data spurted out like blood and internal organs.

‘Then, there were the second, the third, the fourth, and the 101st rune arrays. Flames, frost, venom, distortion, and direct absorption of life force… All kinds of weird attack logic that had never been seen before popped up one after another and launched a marvelous yet difficult-to-defend interplanetary

strike!

“AHHHHHHHHHHH!””

Lu Qingchen was blown into pieces by the subsequent magical attacks and screamed miserably. It was not because the enemy’s absolute strength was much higher than his, but because the unique usage of data, information, and energy was something that he had never seen, or even imagined, before.

Encountering a magical attack in the world of Cultivators? It did not make any sense!

“You are very strong, especially the power named ‘Fuxi’ inside your body.

Watching the colorful ‘fireworks’ around Lu Qingchen, the Fist King finally opened his mouth and said casually, “But right now, you haven’t learned how to control the power yet. Instead, you have been controlled by it and become his slave.

“If you can cultivate for a hundred years and completely absorb this power, and if I am unable to advance during this hundred years, then I am definitely no match for you.”

“But right now…”

BOOM!

A complicated rune array appeared on the face of Lu Qingchen’s giant of light, too, before it exploded brutally. His head was frozen at first, then cracked, and then black smoke and magma spurted out of the cracks.. He could not have looked more miserable!
